Perfect. Now let me create a comprehensive summary document for the user based on all the information I've gathered:

## Comprehensive Subscription System Analysis

I've completed a thorough exploration of the subscription system across the entire codebase. Here's the complete picture:

---

### 1. DATABASE SCHEMA

**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/db/migrations.go`

**Subscriptions Table:**
```sql
CREATE TABLE subscriptions (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    channel_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES notification_channels(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL CHECK (type IN ('source', 'project')),
    source_id UUID REFERENCES sources(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    project_id UUID REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    version_filter TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    CHECK (
        (type = 'source'  AND source_id  IS NOT NULL AND project_id IS NULL) OR
        (type = 'project' AND project_id IS NOT NULL AND source_id  IS NULL)
    )
);
```

**Key Schema Features:**
- **Two subscription types:** `'source'` (raw release notifications) and `'project'` (semantic release notifications)
- **Type-specific constraints:** CHECK constraint enforces mutual exclusivity — a source subscription MUST have `source_id` and NULL `project_id`; vice versa for project subscriptions
- **One ID per subscription type:** Current design enforces that each subscription targets exactly one source OR one project (not multiple)
- **Version filtering:** Optional regex pattern to filter which versions trigger notifications
- **Cascade delete:** Deleting a channel or source cascades deletion of associated subscriptions

**Related Tables:**
- `notification_channels` — Standalone channels (webhooks, Slack, Discord) with `id`, `name`, `type`, `config` (JSONB for credentials/URLs)
- `sources` — Ingestion sources, `project_id` FK ensures each source belongs to one project
- `projects` — Tracked software projects (central entity)

---

### 2. BACKEND API (`internal/api/`)

**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/subscriptions.go`

**HTTP Endpoints (registered in `server.go`):**
```
GET    /api/v1/subscriptions          — List all subscriptions (paginated)
POST   /api/v1/subscriptions          — Create new subscription
GET    /api/v1/subscriptions/{id}     — Get single subscription
PUT    /api/v1/subscriptions/{id}     — Update subscription
DELETE /api/v1/subscriptions/{id}     — Delete subscription
```

**Creation Validation (lines 44-76):**
1. `type` must be exactly `'source'` or `'project'`
2. `channel_id` is required (non-empty)
3. If `type == 'source'`: `source_id` is required
4. If `type == 'project'`: `project_id` is required
5. **No validation enforces one project per subscription** — the constraint is purely in the database CHECK

**Go Struct** (`internal/models/subscription.go`):
```go
type Subscription struct {
	ID            string    `json:"id"`
	ChannelID     string    `json:"channel_id"`
	Type          string    `json:"type"`
	SourceID      *string   `json:"source_id,omitempty"`
	ProjectID     *string   `json:"project_id,omitempty"`
	VersionFilter string    `json:"version_filter,omitempty"`
	CreatedAt     time.Time `json:"created_at"`
}
```

**Store Implementation** (`internal/api/pgstore.go`, lines 288-355):
- `ListSubscriptions()` — Query all rows, paginated by created_at DESC
- `CreateSubscription()` — Single INSERT with RETURNING
- `GetSubscription()` — Query by ID
- `UpdateSubscription()` — UPDATE all fields, return created_at (note: doesn't update created_at)
- `DeleteSubscription()` — DELETE by ID

**Notification Routing Methods** (used by workers):
- `ListSourceSubscriptions(sourceID)` — Query subscriptions WHERE type='source' AND source_id=$1
- `ListProjectSubscriptions(projectID)` — Query subscriptions WHERE type='project' AND project_id=$1

---

### 3. BACKEND MODELS/TYPES

**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/models/subscription.go`

Already shown above. Uses pointers for optional fields (`*string` for SourceID and ProjectID) to allow proper JSON marshaling of null values.

---

### 4. FRONTEND

**Page:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/app/subscriptions/page.tsx`

**UI Features:**
- Table listing all subscriptions with columns: Type, Target (source label or project name), Channel, Version Filter, Actions
- Create dialog with form
- Edit dialog (in-place form)
- Delete confirmation dialog
- Fetches subscriptions, channels, projects, and sources (lazy-loads sources per subscription)

**Form Component:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/subscriptions/subscription-form.tsx`

**Form Logic:**
```typescript
const [type, setType] = useState<"source" | "project">(initial?.type ?? "project");
const [channelId, setChannelId] = useState(initial?.channel_id ?? "");
const [projectId, setProjectId] = useState(initial?.project_id ?? "");
const [sourceId, setSourceId] = useState(initial?.source_id ?? "");
const [versionFilter, setVersionFilter] = useState(initial?.version_filter ?? "");

// On type change: if switching to 'source', fetches sources for the selected project
// Submission: sends only the relevant ID based on type
```

**Key UX:**
- Type dropdown switches between 'Project' and 'Source'
- For 'source' type: requires selecting a project first, then sources dropdown is populated
- For 'project' type: only shows project selector
- Channel is always required (shared UI element)
- Version Filter is optional and accepts regex patterns

---

### 5. NOTIFICATION CHANNELS SYSTEM

**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/channels.go`

**Channels CRUD:**
- GET/POST/PUT/DELETE on `/api/v1/channels`
- POST `/api/v1/channels/{id}/test` — Sends test notification to verify channel works

**Supported Channel Types:**
- `webhook` — Generic HTTP POST
- `slack` — Slack incoming webhooks
- `discord` — Discord webhooks

**Channel Config (JSONB):**
- Webhook: `{ "url": "https://..." }`
- Slack: (parsed by SlackSender)
- Discord: (parsed by DiscordSender)

---

### 6. NOTIFICATION ROUTING FLOW

**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/worker.go`

**When a new source release is created:**
1. Ingestion layer enqueues `NotifyJobArgs{ReleaseID, SourceID}` (transactional outbox pattern)
2. `NotifyWorker.Work()` is invoked by River:
   - Fetches the release
   - Calls `ListSourceSubscriptions(sourceID)` — retrieves ALL subscriptions for this source
   - For each subscription:
     - Gets the channel
     - Looks up the appropriate sender (webhook/slack/discord)
     - Sends notification via `sender.Send(ctx, channel, msg)`
   - Checks agent rules and auto-triggers agent runs if criteria met

**Key Point:** Source subscriptions are resolved at notification time — all matching subscriptions for a source receive notifications.

**Sender Interface:**
```go
type Sender interface {
    Send(ctx context.Context, ch *models.NotificationChannel, msg Notification) error
}
```

**Notification Struct:**
```go
type Notification struct {
    Title   string
    Body    string
    Version string
}
```

---

### 7. CURRENT "ONE PROJECT PER SUBSCRIPTION" CONSTRAINT

**Where it's enforced:**

1. **Database CHECK Constraint** (in `migrations.go`):
   ```sql
   CHECK (
       (type = 'source'  AND source_id  IS NOT NULL AND project_id IS NULL) OR
       (type = 'project' AND project_id IS NOT NULL AND source_id  IS NULL)
   )
   ```
   This ensures a subscription must target exactly ONE source OR ONE project — never both, never neither.

2. **Implicit in schema:** 
   - Source has `project_id` FK → each source belongs to exactly one project
   - When creating a source-type subscription with `source_id`, the project is implicit (derive via JOIN with sources)
   - When creating a project-type subscription, the project is explicit

3. **No explicit validation in API:** The API doesn't validate "this source belongs to this project" — it leaves that to the database FK constraints.

**This constraint is ONLY enforced in the database, not at the API level.** The API accepts:
- Source subscriptions: `{channel_id, type: "source", source_id}`
- Project subscriptions: `{channel_id, type: "project", project_id}`

But there's no validation that the source belongs to the specified project or vice versa.

---

### 8. FRONTEND API TYPES

**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/lib/api/types.ts`

```typescript
export interface Subscription {
  id: string;
  channel_id: string;
  type: "source" | "project";
  source_id?: string;
  project_id?: string;
  version_filter?: string;
  created_at: string;
}

export interface SubscriptionInput {
  channel_id: string;
  type: "source" | "project";
  source_id?: string;
  project_id?: string;
  version_filter?: string;
}
```

**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/lib/api/client.ts`

```typescript
export const subscriptions = {
  list: () => request<ApiResponse<Subscription[]>>("/subscriptions"),
  get: (id: string) => request<ApiResponse<Subscription>>(`/subscriptions/${id}`),
  create: (input: SubscriptionInput) => request<ApiResponse<Subscription>>("/subscriptions", { method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify(input) }),
  update: (id: string, input: SubscriptionInput) => request<ApiResponse<Subscription>>(`/subscriptions/{id}`, { method: "PUT", body: JSON.stringify(input) }),
  delete: (id: string) => request<ApiResponse<null>>(`/subscriptions/{id}`, { method: "DELETE" }),
};
```

---

### 9. KEY DESIGN DECISIONS

**From DESIGN.md:**
- **Separation of concerns:** Notification channels are standalone, reusable entities. Multiple subscriptions can reference the same channel.
- **Two subscription types:** Source subscriptions are low-latency (notify immediately on raw release). Project subscriptions wait for AI analysis to complete.
- **Version filtering:** Optional regex allows filtering within a single subscription (e.g., only notify on production releases).
- **Transactional integrity:** Release insert + notify job enqueue happen in same transaction — zero-loss guarantee.

---

### 10. FILES SUMMARY

| File | Purpose |
|------|---------|
| `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/db/migrations.go` | Database schema definition (subscriptions table + triggers) |
| `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/models/subscription.go` | Go struct definitions |
| `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/subscriptions.go` | HTTP handler (CRUD endpoints, validation) |
| `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/pgstore.go` | PostgreSQL persistence (lines 288-355 for subscriptions) |
| `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/channels.go` | Notification channel CRUD + test |
| `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/worker.go` | NotifyWorker — resolves subscriptions, sends notifications |
| `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/webhook.go` | Webhook sender implementation |

| `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/lib/api/client.ts` | Frontend API client (subscriptions namespace) |

---

This is a complete picture of the current subscription system. The key architectural feature is the separation of channels from subscriptions, allowing many-to-many routing while the database CHECK constraint ensures each subscription targets exactly one source OR one project (never both).

> AGENT

Great, multi-select in the create form with batch creation behind the scenes. The DB schema stays the same. Here are the approaches:

## Approach 1: Frontend-only batch create (Recommended)

- Change the subscription form's project/source selector from single-select to multi-select
- On submit, the frontend loops and calls `POST /api/v1/subscriptions` once per selected project/source
- **No backend changes at all**
- Trade-off: Multiple HTTP requests, but subscriptions are lightweight and this is a rare action. Non-atomic (some could fail).

## Approach 2: Backend batch endpoint

- Add a new `POST /api/v1/subscriptions/batch` endpoint that accepts an array of project/source IDs + one channel ID
- Backend creates all subscriptions in a single transaction (atomic)
- Frontend sends one request
- Trade-off: More backend work, but atomic and cleaner. Slightly more complex API.

## Approach 3: Backend batch + frontend multi-select

- Same as Approach 2, but also add a "select all" checkbox in the UI
- Trade-off: Most feature-complete but most work.

**My recommendation: Approach 2** — A batch endpoint is minimal backend work (one new handler, one transaction), gives you atomicity, and keeps the frontend clean with a single request. The "select all" from Approach 3 is easy to add later in the UI if needed.

     8→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/models"

    10→
    11→// SubscriptionsStore defines the persistence operations for subscriptions.
    12→type SubscriptionsStore interface {
    13→	ListSubscriptions(ctx context.Context, page, perPage int) ([]models.Subscription, int, error)
    14→	CreateSubscription(ctx context.Context, sub *models.Subscription) error
    15→	GetSubscription(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.Subscription, error)
    16→	UpdateSubscription(ctx context.Context, id string, sub *models.Subscription) error
    17→	DeleteSubscription(ctx context.Context, id string) error
    18→}
    19→
    20→// SubscriptionsHandler implements HTTP handlers for the /subscriptions resource.
    21→type SubscriptionsHandler struct {
    22→	store SubscriptionsStore
    23→}
    24→
    25→// NewSubscriptionsHandler returns a new SubscriptionsHandler.
    26→func NewSubscriptionsHandler(store SubscriptionsStore) *SubscriptionsHandler {
    27→	return &SubscriptionsHandler{store: store}
    28→}
    29→
    30→// List handles GET /subscriptions — returns a paginated list of subscriptions.
    31→func (h *SubscriptionsHandler) List(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    32→	page, perPage := ParsePagination(r)
    33→	subs, total, err := h.store.ListSubscriptions(r.Context(), page, perPage)
    34→	if err != nil {
    35→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, "internal_error", "Failed to list subscriptions")
    36→		return
    37→	}
    38→	if subs == nil {
    39→		subs = []models.Subscription{}
    40→	}
    41→	RespondList(w, r, http.StatusOK, subs, page, perPage, total)
    42→}
    43→
    44→// Create handles POST /subscriptions — creates a new subscription.
    45→func (h *SubscriptionsHandler) Create(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    46→	var sub models.Subscription
    47→	if err := DecodeJSON(r, &sub); err != nil {
    48→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Invalid JSON body")
    49→		return
    50→	}
    51→	sub.Type = strings.TrimSpace(sub.Type)
    52→	sub.ChannelID = strings.TrimSpace(sub.ChannelID)
    53→	// Validate type is source or project.
    54→	if sub.Type != "source" && sub.Type != "project" {
    55→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "validation_error", "type must be 'source' or 'project'")
    56→		return
    57→	}
    58→	if sub.ChannelID == "" {
    59→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "validation_error", "channel_id is required")
    60→		return
    61→	}
    62→	// Validate that the corresponding ID is set for the type.
    63→	if sub.Type == "source" && (sub.SourceID == nil || *sub.SourceID == "") {
    64→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "validation_error", "source_id is required when type is 'source'")
    65→		return
    66→	}
    67→	if sub.Type == "project" && (sub.ProjectID == nil || *sub.ProjectID == "") {
    68→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "validation_error", "project_id is required when type is 'project'")
    69→		return
    70→	}
    71→	if err := h.store.CreateSubscription(r.Context(), &sub); err != nil {
    72→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, "internal_error", "Failed to create subscription")
    73→		return
    74→	}
    75→	RespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusCreated, sub)
    76→}
    77→
    78→// Get handles GET /subscriptions/{id} — returns a single subscription.
    79→func (h *SubscriptionsHandler) Get(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    80→	id := r.PathValue("id")
    81→	if id == "" {
    82→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Invalid subscription ID")
    83→		return
    84→	}
    85→	sub, err := h.store.GetSubscription(r.Context(), id)
    86→	if err != nil {
    87→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusNotFound, "not_found", "Subscription not found")
    88→		return
    89→	}
    90→	RespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusOK, sub)
    91→}
    92→
    93→// Update handles PUT /subscriptions/{id} — updates an existing subscription.
    94→func (h *SubscriptionsHandler) Update(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    95→	id := r.PathValue("id")
    96→	if id == "" {
    97→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Invalid subscription ID")
    98→		return
    99→	}
   100→	var sub models.Subscription
   101→	if err := DecodeJSON(r, &sub); err != nil {
   102→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Invalid JSON body")
   103→		return
   104→	}
   105→	sub.Type = strings.TrimSpace(sub.Type)
   106→	sub.ChannelID = strings.TrimSpace(sub.ChannelID)
   107→	if sub.Type != "source" && sub.Type != "project" {
   108→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "validation_error", "type must be 'source' or 'project'")
   109→		return
   110→	}
   111→	if sub.ChannelID == "" {
   112→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "validation_error", "channel_id is required")
   113→		return
   114→	}
   115→	if sub.Type == "source" && (sub.SourceID == nil || *sub.SourceID == "") {
   116→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "validation_error", "source_id is required when type is 'source'")
   117→		return
   118→	}
   119→	if sub.Type == "project" && (sub.ProjectID == nil || *sub.ProjectID == "") {
   120→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "validation_error", "project_id is required when type is 'project'")
   121→		return
   122→	}
   123→	if err := h.store.UpdateSubscription(r.Context(), id, &sub); err != nil {
   124→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusNotFound, "not_found", "Subscription not found")
   125→		return
   126→	}
   127→	sub.ID = id
   128→	RespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusOK, sub)
   129→}
   130→
   131→// Delete handles DELETE /subscriptions/{id} — deletes a subscription.
   132→func (h *SubscriptionsHandler) Delete(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   133→	id := r.PathValue("id")
   134→	if id == "" {
   135→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Invalid subscription ID")
   136→		return
   137→	}
   138→	if err := h.store.DeleteSubscription(r.Context(), id); err != nil {
   139→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusNotFound, "not_found", "Subscription not found")
   140→		return
   141→	}
   142→	w.WriteHeader(http.StatusNoContent)
   143→}
   144→

     1→"use client";
     2→
     3→import { useState } from "react";
     4→import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
     5→import useSWR from "swr";
     6→import { projects as projectsApi, channels as channelsApi, sources as sourcesApi } from "@/lib/api/client";
     7→import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
     8→import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
     9→import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
    10→import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
    11→import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "@/components/ui/select";
    12→import type { Subscription, SubscriptionInput } from "@/lib/api/types";
    13→
    14→interface SubscriptionFormProps {
    15→  initial?: Subscription;
    16→  onSubmit: (input: SubscriptionInput) => Promise<void>;
    17→  title: string;
    18→  onSuccess?: () => void;
    19→  onCancel?: () => void;
    20→}
    21→
    22→export function SubscriptionForm({ initial, onSubmit, title, onSuccess, onCancel }: SubscriptionFormProps) {
    23→  const router = useRouter();
    24→  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
    25→  const [error, setError] = useState("");
    26→  const [type, setType] = useState<"source" | "project">(initial?.type ?? "project");
    27→  const [channelId, setChannelId] = useState(initial?.channel_id ?? "");
    28→  const [projectId, setProjectId] = useState(initial?.project_id ?? "");
    29→  const [sourceId, setSourceId] = useState(initial?.source_id ?? "");
    30→  const [versionFilter, setVersionFilter] = useState(initial?.version_filter ?? "");
    31→
    32→  const { data: projectsData } = useSWR("projects-for-sub", () => projectsApi.list());
    33→  const { data: channelsData } = useSWR("channels-for-sub", () => channelsApi.list());
    34→  const { data: sourcesData } = useSWR(
    35→    type === "source" && projectId ? `sources-for-sub-${projectId}` : null,
    36→    () => sourcesApi.listByProject(projectId)
    37→  );
    38→
    39→  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    40→    e.preventDefault();
    41→    setError("");
    42→    setSaving(true);
    43→    try {
    44→      await onSubmit({
    45→        channel_id: channelId,
    46→        type,
    47→        source_id: type === "source" ? sourceId : undefined,
    48→        project_id: type === "project" ? projectId : undefined,
    49→        version_filter: versionFilter || undefined,
    50→      });
    51→      if (onSuccess) {
    52→        onSuccess();
    53→      } else {
    54→        router.push("/subscriptions");
    55→      }
    56→    } catch (err: unknown) {
    57→      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to save");
    58→    } finally {
    59→      setSaving(false);
    60→    }
    61→  };
    62→
    63→  const formContent = (
    64→    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
    65→      {error && <div className="rounded-md bg-red-50 p-3 text-sm text-red-700">{error}</div>}
    66→      <div className="space-y-2">
    67→        <Label>Subscription Type</Label>
    68→        <Select value={type} onValueChange={(v) => setType(v as "source" | "project")}>
    69→          <SelectTrigger><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger>
    70→          <SelectContent>
    71→            <SelectItem value="project">Project</SelectItem>
    72→            <SelectItem value="source">Source</SelectItem>
    73→          </SelectContent>
    74→        </Select>
    75→      </div>
    76→      {type === "project" && (
    77→        <div className="space-y-2">
    78→          <Label>Project</Label>
    79→          <Select value={projectId} onValueChange={setProjectId} required>
    80→            <SelectTrigger><SelectValue placeholder="Select project" /></SelectTrigger>
    81→            <SelectContent>
    82→              {projectsData?.data.map((p) => (
    83→                <SelectItem key={p.id} value={p.id}>{p.name}</SelectItem>
    84→              ))}
    85→            </SelectContent>
    86→          </Select>
    87→        </div>
    88→      )}
    89→      {type === "source" && (
    90→        <>
    91→          <div className="space-y-2">
    92→            <Label>Project (to list sources)</Label>
    93→            <Select value={projectId} onValueChange={(v) => { setProjectId(v); setSourceId(""); }}>
    94→              <SelectTrigger><SelectValue placeholder="Select project" /></SelectTrigger>
    95→              <SelectContent>
    96→                {projectsData?.data.map((p) => (
    97→                  <SelectItem key={p.id} value={p.id}>{p.name}</SelectItem>
    98→                ))}
    99→              </SelectContent>
   100→            </Select>
   101→          </div>
   102→          <div className="space-y-2">
   103→            <Label>Source</Label>
   104→            <Select value={sourceId} onValueChange={setSourceId} required>
   105→              <SelectTrigger><SelectValue placeholder="Select source" /></SelectTrigger>
   106→              <SelectContent>
   107→                {sourcesData?.data.map((s) => (
   108→                  <SelectItem key={s.id} value={s.id}>{s.provider}: {s.repository}</SelectItem>
   109→                ))}
   110→              </SelectContent>
   111→            </Select>
   112→          </div>
   113→        </>
   114→      )}
   115→      <div className="space-y-2">
   116→        <Label>Notification Channel</Label>
   117→        <Select value={channelId} onValueChange={setChannelId} required>
   118→          <SelectTrigger><SelectValue placeholder="Select channel" /></SelectTrigger>
   119→          <SelectContent>
   120→            {channelsData?.data.map((ch) => (
   121→              <SelectItem key={ch.id} value={ch.id}>{ch.name} ({ch.type})</SelectItem>
   122→            ))}
   123→          </SelectContent>
   124→        </Select>
   125→      </div>
   126→      <div className="space-y-2">
   127→        <Label htmlFor="version_filter">Version Filter (regex, optional)</Label>
   128→        <Input id="version_filter" value={versionFilter} onChange={(e) => setVersionFilter(e.target.value)} placeholder='e.g. ^v\d+\.\d+\.\d+$' />
   129→      </div>
   130→      <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
   131→        <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={() => onCancel ? onCancel() : router.back()}>Cancel</Button>
   132→        <Button type="submit" disabled={saving}>{saving ? "Saving..." : "Save"}</Button>
   133→      </div>
   134→    </form>
   135→  );
   136→
   137→  if (onSuccess) {
   138→    return formContent;
   139→  }
   140→
   141→  return (
   142→    <Card className="mx-auto max-w-2xl">
   143→      <CardHeader><CardTitle>{title}</CardTitle></CardHeader>
   144→      <CardContent>
   145→        {formContent}
   146→      </CardContent>
   147→    </Card>
   148→  );
   149→}
   150→

    11→// Dependencies holds all external dependencies required for registering API routes.
    12→type Dependencies struct {
    13→	DB                    *pgxpool.Pool
    14→	ProjectsStore         ProjectsStore
    15→	ReleasesStore         ReleasesStore
    16→	SubscriptionsStore    SubscriptionsStore
    17→	SourcesStore          SourcesStore

    28→}
    29→
    30→// RegisterRoutes registers all API v1 routes on the given ServeMux.
    31→func RegisterRoutes(mux *http.ServeMux, deps Dependencies) {
    32→	// Public chain: no auth required.
    33→	publicChain := Chain(RequestID, Logger, Recovery)
    34→
    35→	// Authenticated chain: includes auth unless NoAuth is set.
    36→	chain := publicChain
    37→	if !deps.NoAuth {
    38→		chain = Chain(RequestID, Logger, Recovery, RateLimit(10, 20), Auth(deps.KeyStore))
    39→	}
    40→
    41→	// Projects (CRUD)
    42→	projects := NewProjectsHandler(deps.ProjectsStore)
    43→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/projects", chain(http.HandlerFunc(projects.List)))
    44→	mux.Handle("POST /api/v1/projects", chain(http.HandlerFunc(projects.Create)))
    45→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/projects/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(projects.Get)))
    46→	mux.Handle("PUT /api/v1/projects/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(projects.Update)))
    47→	mux.Handle("DELETE /api/v1/projects/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(projects.Delete)))
    48→
    49→	// Sources (nested under projects)
    50→	sources := NewSourcesHandler(deps.SourcesStore, deps.IngestionService, deps.HTTPClient)

   288→// --- SubscriptionsStore ---
   289→
   290→func (s *PgStore) ListSubscriptions(ctx context.Context, page, perPage int) ([]models.Subscription, int, error) {
   291→	var total int
   292→	err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM subscriptions`).Scan(&total)
   293→	if err != nil {
   294→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("count subscriptions: %w", err)
   295→	}
   296→	offset := (page - 1) * perPage
   297→	rows, err := s.pool.Query(ctx,
   298→		`SELECT id, channel_id, type, source_id, project_id, COALESCE(version_filter,''), created_at
   299→		 FROM subscriptions ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT $1 OFFSET $2`, perPage, offset)
   300→	if err != nil {
   301→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("list subscriptions: %w", err)
   302→	}
   303→	defer rows.Close()
   304→	var subs []models.Subscription
   305→	for rows.Next() {
   306→		var sub models.Subscription
   307→		if err := rows.Scan(&sub.ID, &sub.ChannelID, &sub.Type, &sub.SourceID,
   308→			&sub.ProjectID, &sub.VersionFilter, &sub.CreatedAt); err != nil {
   309→			return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("scan subscription: %w", err)
   310→		}
   311→		subs = append(subs, sub)
   312→	}
   313→	return subs, total, nil
   314→}
   315→
   316→func (s *PgStore) CreateSubscription(ctx context.Context, sub *models.Subscription) error {
   317→	return s.pool.QueryRow(ctx,
   318→		`INSERT INTO subscriptions (channel_id, type, source_id, project_id, version_filter)
   319→		 VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5)
   320→		 RETURNING id, created_at`,
   321→		sub.ChannelID, sub.Type, sub.SourceID, sub.ProjectID, sub.VersionFilter,
   322→	).Scan(&sub.ID, &sub.CreatedAt)
   323→}
   324→
   325→func (s *PgStore) GetSubscription(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.Subscription, error) {
   326→	var sub models.Subscription
   327→	err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx,
   328→		`SELECT id, channel_id, type, source_id, project_id, COALESCE(version_filter,''), created_at
   329→		 FROM subscriptions WHERE id = $1`, id,
   330→	).Scan(&sub.ID, &sub.ChannelID, &sub.Type, &sub.SourceID,
   331→		&sub.ProjectID, &sub.VersionFilter, &sub.CreatedAt)
   332→	if err != nil {
   333→		return nil, err
   334→	}
   335→	return &sub, nil
   336→}
   337→
   338→func (s *PgStore) UpdateSubscription(ctx context.Context, id string, sub *models.Subscription) error {
   339→	return s.pool.QueryRow(ctx,
   340→		`UPDATE subscriptions SET channel_id=$1, type=$2, source_id=$3, project_id=$4, version_filter=$5
   341→		 WHERE id=$6 RETURNING created_at`,
   342→		sub.ChannelID, sub.Type, sub.SourceID, sub.ProjectID, sub.VersionFilter, id,
   343→	).Scan(&sub.CreatedAt)
   344→}
   345→
   346→func (s *PgStore) DeleteSubscription(ctx context.Context, id string) error {
   347→	tag, err := s.pool.Exec(ctx, `DELETE FROM subscriptions WHERE id = $1`, id)
   348→	if err != nil {
   349→		return err
   350→	}
   351→	if tag.RowsAffected() == 0 {
   352→		return fmt.Errorf("not found")
   353→	}
   354→	return nil
   355→}
   356→

> AGENT

Now I have the full picture. Here's the design:

## Design: Multi-Select Subscription Creation

### Problem
To subscribe all projects to the same channel, you must create N subscriptions manually, one at a time.

### Solution
Add a `POST /api/v1/subscriptions/batch` endpoint and update the frontend form to support multi-select for projects/sources.

### Backend Changes

**New endpoint:** `POST /api/v1/subscriptions/batch`

Request body:
```json
{
  "channel_id": "uuid",
  "type": "project",
  "project_ids": ["uuid1", "uuid2", "uuid3"],
  "version_filter": "^v\\d+"
}
```
Or for source type:
```json
{
  "channel_id": "uuid",
  "type": "source",
  "source_ids": ["uuid1", "uuid2"],
  "version_filter": ""
}
```

Response: array of created subscriptions. All created in a single DB transaction.

**Store layer:** Add `CreateSubscriptions(ctx, subs []*Subscription) error` that does a batch INSERT in one transaction.

**No schema changes.** Each row is still one subscription targeting one project/source. We're just creating multiple rows in one API call.

### Frontend Changes

**Subscription form (`subscription-form.tsx`):**
- When creating (no `initial`), project/source selector becomes multi-select (checkboxes)
- When editing (`initial` provided), keeps single-select behavior (you're editing one subscription)
- On create submit: calls `/subscriptions/batch` with all selected IDs
- Add "Select All" convenience checkbox

**No changes to:** listing page, edit flow, delete flow, notification routing.

### What stays the same
- DB schema (no migration)
- Existing single-create endpoint (backward compatible)
- Edit/delete endpoints
- Notification routing logic

Does this design look right to you?

{
  "file_path": "/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/docs/plans/2026-03-01-multi-select-subscriptions-design.md",
  "content": "# Multi-Select Subscription Creation\n\n## Problem\n\nCreating subscriptions is 1:1 (one project/source per subscription). To publish all projects to the same channel, users must create N subscriptions manually.\n\n## Solution\n\nAdd `POST /api/v1/subscriptions/batch` endpoint + multi-select UI in the create form. No DB schema changes — each row still targets one project/source; the batch endpoint creates multiple rows in a single transaction.\n\n## Backend\n\n### New Endpoint: `POST /api/v1/subscriptions/batch`\n\n**Request (project type):**\n```json\n{\n  \"channel_id\": \"uuid\",\n  \"type\": \"project\",\n  \"project_ids\": [\"uuid1\", \"uuid2\", \"uuid3\"],\n  \"version_filter\": \"^v\\\\d+\"\n}\n```\n\n**Request (source type):**\n```json\n{\n  \"channel_id\": \"uuid\",\n  \"type\": \"source\",\n  \"source_ids\": [\"uuid1\", \"uuid2\"],\n  \"version_filter\": \"\"\n}\n```\n\n**Response:** `201 Created` with array of created subscriptions.\n\n**Validation:**\n- `type` must be `source` or `project`\n- `channel_id` required\n- `project_ids` required (non-empty) when type is `project`\n- `source_ids` required (non-empty) when type is `source`\n\n### Store Layer\n\nAdd `CreateSubscriptionBatch(ctx, subs []models.Subscription) ([]models.Subscription, error)` to `PgStore`. Uses a single transaction with batch INSERT + RETURNING.\n\n### Files Changed\n\n- `internal/api/subscriptions.go` — add `BatchCreate` handler, `BatchSubscriptionInput` struct\n- `internal/api/pgstore.go` — add `CreateSubscriptionBatch` method\n- `internal/api/server.go` — register `POST /api/v1/subscriptions/batch`\n\n## Frontend\n\n### Subscription Form (`web/components/subscriptions/subscription-form.tsx`)\n\n- **Create mode** (no `initial`): project/source selector becomes multi-select with checkboxes\n- **Edit mode** (`initial` provided): single-select, unchanged\n- On submit in create mode: calls `POST /subscriptions/batch` with selected IDs\n- Add \"Select All\" checkbox convenience\n\n### API Client (`web/lib/api/client.ts`, `web/lib/api/types.ts`)\n\n- Add `BatchSubscriptionInput` type\n- Add `subscriptions.batchCreate()` method\n\n## Unchanged\n\n- Database schema (no migration needed)\n- Existing `POST /subscriptions` (backward compatible)\n- Edit/delete endpoints\n- Notification routing logic\n- Subscription list page\n"
}

    22→const BASE = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL || "/api/v1";
    23→
    24→async function request<T>(path: string, init?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {
    25→  const res = await fetch(`${BASE}${path}`, {
    26→    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...init?.headers },
    27→    ...init,
    28→  });
    29→  if (!res.ok) {
    30→    const body = await res.json().catch(() => null);
    31→    throw new Error(body?.error?.message ?? `Request failed: ${res.status}`);
    32→  }
    33→  if (res.status === 204) return null as T;
    34→  return res.json();
    35→}
    36→
    37→// --- Projects ---
    38→
    39→export const projects = {
    40→  list: (page = 1, perPage = 25) =>
    41→    request<ApiResponse<Project[]>>(`/projects?page=${page}&per_page=${perPage}`),
    42→  get: (id: string) =>
    43→    request<ApiResponse<Project>>(`/projects/${id}`),
    44→  create: (input: ProjectInput) =>
    45→    request<ApiResponse<Project>>("/projects", {
    46→      method: "POST",
    47→      body: JSON.stringify(input),
    48→    }),
    49→  update: (id: string, input: ProjectInput) =>
    50→    request<ApiResponse<Project>>(`/projects/${id}`, {
    51→      method: "PUT",
    52→      body: JSON.stringify(input),
    53→    }),
    54→  delete: (id: string) =>
    55→    request<ApiResponse<null>>(`/projects/${id}`, { method: "DELETE" }),
    56→};

    12→import { Plus, Pencil, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
    13→import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from "@/components/ui/dialog";
    14→import { ConfirmDialog } from "@/components/ui/confirm-dialog";
    15→import { SubscriptionForm } from "@/components/subscriptions/subscription-form";
    16→
    17→const SUB_TYPE_COLORS: Record<string, { bg: string; text: string }> = {
    18→  source: { bg: "#1a1a1a", text: "#ffffff" },
    19→  project: { bg: "#2563eb", text: "#ffffff" },
    20→};
    21→
    22→function SubTypeBadge({ type }: { type: string }) {
    23→  const colors = SUB_TYPE_COLORS[type] ?? { bg: "#6b7280", text: "#ffffff" };
    24→  return (
    25→    <span
    26→      className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5"
    27→      style={{
    28→        backgroundColor: colors.bg,
    29→        color: colors.text,
    30→        fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
    31→        fontSize: "12px",
    32→        fontWeight: 500,
    33→        lineHeight: "16px",
    34→      }}
    35→    >
    36→      {type}
    37→    </span>
    38→  );
    39→}
    40→
    41→export default function SubscriptionsPage() {
    42→  const { data, isLoading } = useSWR("subscriptions", () => subsApi.list());
    43→  const { data: channelsData } = useSWR("channels-for-sub-list", () =>
    44→    channelsApi.list()
    45→  );
    46→  const { data: projectsData } = useSWR("projects-for-sub-list", () =>
    47→    projectsApi.list(1, 100)
    48→  );
    49→
    50→  // Collect unique source IDs from subscriptions and fetch each one
    51→  const sourceIds = useMemo(
    52→    () =>
    53→      Array.from(
    54→        new Set(
    55→          (data?.data ?? [])
    56→            .filter((s) => s.type === "source" && s.source_id)
    57→            .map((s) => s.source_id!)
    58→        )
    59→      ),
    60→    [data]
    61→  );
    62→  const { data: sourcesMap } = useSWR(
    63→    sourceIds.length > 0 ? `sources-for-sub-list-${sourceIds.join(",")}` : null,
    64→    async () => {
    65→      const results = await Promise.all(
    66→        sourceIds.map((id) => sourcesApi.get(id).catch(() => null))
    67→      );
    68→      const map: Record<string, Source> = {};
    69→      for (const r of results) {
    70→        if (r?.data) map[r.data.id] = r.data;
    71→      }
    72→      return map;
    73→    }
    74→  );
    75→
    76→  const [createOpen, setCreateOpen] = useState(false);
    77→  const [editingSub, setEditingSub] = useState<Subscription | null>(null);
    78→  const [deletingId, setDeletingId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    79→
    80→  const getChannelName = (id: string) =>
    81→    channelsData?.data.find((c) => c.id === id)?.name ?? id;
    82→
    83→  const getProjectName = (id: string) =>
    84→    projectsData?.data.find((p) => p.id === id)?.name ?? id;
    85→
    86→  const getSourceLabel = (id: string) => {
    87→    const source = sourcesMap?.[id];
    88→    return source ? `${source.provider}: ${source.repository}` : id;
    89→  };
    90→
    91→  const subscriptions = data?.data ?? [];
    92→
    93→  return (
    94→    <div className="space-y-6">
    95→      {/* Header */}
    96→      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
    97→        <h1
    98→          style={{
    99→            fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
   100→            fontSize: "24px",
   101→            fontWeight: 700,
   102→            color: "#111113",
   103→          }}
   104→        >
   105→          Subscriptions
   106→        </h1>
   107→        <button
   108→          onClick={() => setCreateOpen(true)}
   109→          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md px-3.5 py-2 transition-colors hover:opacity-90"
   110→          style={{
   111→            backgroundColor: "#e8601a",
   112→            color: "#ffffff",
   113→            fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   114→            fontSize: "13px",
   115→            fontWeight: 500,
   116→          }}
   117→        >
   118→          <Plus className="h-4 w-4" />
   119→          New Subscription
   120→        </button>
   121→      </div>
   122→
   123→      {/* Table card */}
   124→      <div
   125→        className="overflow-hidden rounded-lg bg-white"
   126→        style={{ border: "1px solid #e8e8e5" }}
   127→      >
   128→        {isLoading ? (
   129→          <div
   130→            className="py-16 text-center"
   131→            style={{
   132→              fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   133→              fontSize: "13px",
   134→              color: "#6b7280",
   135→            }}
   136→          >
   137→            Loading...
   138→          </div>
   139→        ) : subscriptions.length === 0 ? (
   140→          <div className="py-16 text-center">
   141→            <p
   142→              style={{
   143→                fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
   144→                fontStyle: "italic",
   145→                fontSize: "15px",
   146→                color: "#9ca3af",
   147→              }}
   148→            >
   149→              No subscriptions configured yet
   150→            </p>
   151→          </div>
   152→        ) : (
   153→          <table className="w-full">
   154→            <thead>
   155→              <tr style={{ backgroundColor: "#fafaf9" }}>
   156→                {["Type", "Target", "Channel", "Version Filter", "Actions"].map(
   157→                  (heading) => (
   158→                    <th
   159→                      key={heading}
   160→                      className="px-5 py-3 text-left"
   161→                      style={{
   162→                        fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   163→                        fontSize: "11px",
   164→                        fontWeight: 500,
   165→                        textTransform: "uppercase",
   166→                        letterSpacing: "0.08em",
   167→                        color: "#9ca3af",
   168→                        borderBottom: "1px solid #e8e8e5",
   169→                      }}
   170→                    >
   171→                      {heading}
   172→                    </th>
   173→                  )
   174→                )}
   175→              </tr>
   176→            </thead>
   177→            <tbody>
   178→              {subscriptions.map((sub) => (
   179→                <tr
   180→                  key={sub.id}
   181→                  className="transition-colors hover:bg-[#fafaf9]"
   182→                  style={{ borderBottom: "1px solid #e8e8e5" }}
   183→                >
   184→                  {/* Type */}
   185→                  <td className="px-5 py-3.5">
   186→                    <SubTypeBadge type={sub.type} />
   187→                  </td>
   188→
   189→                  {/* Target */}
   190→                  <td
   191→                    className="px-5 py-3.5"
   192→                    style={{
   193→                      fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   194→                      fontSize: "14px",
   195→                      fontWeight: 500,
   196→                      color: "#111113",
   197→                    }}
   198→                  >
   199→                    {sub.type === "source"
   200→                      ? getSourceLabel(sub.source_id!)
   201→                      : getProjectName(sub.project_id!)}
   202→                  </td>
   203→
   204→                  {/* Channel */}
   205→                  <td
   206→                    className="px-5 py-3.5"
   207→                    style={{
   208→                      fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   209→                      fontSize: "14px",
   210→                      color: "#111113",
   211→                    }}
   212→                  >
   213→                    {getChannelName(sub.channel_id)}
   214→                  </td>
   215→
   216→                  {/* Version Filter */}
   217→                  <td
   218→                    className="px-5 py-3.5"
   219→                    style={{
   220→                      fontFamily: sub.version_filter
   221→                        ? "'JetBrains Mono', monospace"
   222→                        : "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   223→                      fontSize: "13px",
   224→                      color: sub.version_filter ? "#111113" : "#9ca3af",
   225→                    }}
   226→                  >
   227→                    {sub.version_filter || "\u2014"}
   228→                  </td>
   229→
   230→                  {/* Actions */}
   231→                  <td className="px-5 py-3.5">
   232→                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   233→                      <button
   234→                        onClick={() => setEditingSub(sub)}
   235→                        className="rounded p-1 text-[#9ca3af] transition-colors hover:bg-[#f3f3f1] hover:text-[#111113]"
   236→                      >
   237→                        <Pencil className="h-4 w-4" />
   238→                      </button>
   239→                      <button
   240→                        onClick={() => setDeletingId(sub.id)}
   241→                        className="rounded p-1 text-[#9ca3af] transition-colors hover:bg-red-50 hover:text-red-600"
   242→                      >
   243→                        <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
   244→                      </button>
   245→                    </div>
   246→                  </td>
   247→                </tr>
   248→              ))}
   249→            </tbody>
   250→          </table>
   251→        )}
   252→      </div>
   253→
   254→      {/* Create dialog */}
   255→      <Dialog open={createOpen} onOpenChange={setCreateOpen}>
   256→        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-lg">
   257→          <DialogHeader>
   258→            <DialogTitle>Create Subscription</DialogTitle>
   259→          </DialogHeader>
   260→          <SubscriptionForm
   261→            title="Create Subscription"
   262→            onSubmit={async (input) => {
   263→              await subsApi.create(input);
   264→            }}
   265→            onSuccess={() => {
   266→              setCreateOpen(false);
   267→              mutate("subscriptions");
   268→            }}
   269→            onCancel={() => setCreateOpen(false)}
   270→          />
   271→        </DialogContent>
   272→      </Dialog>
   273→
   274→      {/* Edit dialog */}
   275→      <Dialog
   276→        open={!!editingSub}
   277→        onOpenChange={(open) => {
   278→          if (!open) setEditingSub(null);
   279→        }}
   280→      >
   281→        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-lg">
   282→          <DialogHeader>
   283→            <DialogTitle>Edit Subscription</DialogTitle>
   284→          </DialogHeader>
   285→          {editingSub && (
   286→            <SubscriptionForm
   287→              key={editingSub.id}
   288→              title="Edit Subscription"
   289→              initial={editingSub}
   290→              onSubmit={async (input) => {
   291→                await subsApi.update(editingSub.id, input);
   292→              }}
   293→              onSuccess={() => {
   294→                setEditingSub(null);
   295→                mutate("subscriptions");
   296→              }}
   297→              onCancel={() => setEditingSub(null)}
   298→            />
   299→          )}
   300→        </DialogContent>
   301→      </Dialog>
   302→
   303→      {/* Delete dialog */}
   304→      <ConfirmDialog
   305→        open={!!deletingId}
   306→        onOpenChange={(open) => {
   307→          if (!open) setDeletingId(null);
   308→        }}
   309→        title="Delete Subscription"
   310→        description="This will permanently delete this subscription. This cannot be undone."
   311→        onConfirm={async () => {
   312→          if (deletingId) {
   313→            await subsApi.delete(deletingId);
   314→            mutate("subscriptions");
   315→          }
   316→        }}
   317→      />
   318→    </div>
   319→  );
   320→}
   321→

    15→
    16→// mockSubscriptionsStore implements SubscriptionsStore for testing.
    17→type mockSubscriptionsStore struct {
    18→	subscriptions []models.Subscription
    19→	created       *models.Subscription
    20→	updated       *models.Subscription
    21→	deleted       string
    22→	listErr       error
    23→	createErr     error
    24→	getErr        error
    25→	updateErr     error
    26→	deleteErr     error
    27→}
    28→
    29→func (m *mockSubscriptionsStore) ListSubscriptions(_ context.Context, page, perPage int) ([]models.Subscription, int, error) {
    30→	if m.listErr != nil {
    31→		return nil, 0, m.listErr
    32→	}
    33→	return m.subscriptions, len(m.subscriptions), nil
    34→}
    35→
    36→func (m *mockSubscriptionsStore) CreateSubscription(_ context.Context, sub *models.Subscription) error {
    37→	if m.createErr != nil {
    38→		return m.createErr
    39→	}
    40→	sub.ID = "sub-001"
    41→	sub.CreatedAt = time.Date(2026, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
    42→	m.created = sub
    43→	return nil
    44→}
    45→
    46→func (m *mockSubscriptionsStore) GetSubscription(_ context.Context, id string) (*models.Subscription, error) {
    47→	if m.getErr != nil {
    48→		return nil, m.getErr
    49→	}
    50→	for i := range m.subscriptions {
    51→		if m.subscriptions[i].ID == id {
    52→			return &m.subscriptions[i], nil
    53→		}
    54→	}
    55→	return nil, fmt.Errorf("not found")
    56→}
    57→
    58→func (m *mockSubscriptionsStore) UpdateSubscription(_ context.Context, id string, sub *models.Subscription) error {
    59→	if m.updateErr != nil {
    60→		return m.updateErr
    61→	}
    62→	for i := range m.subscriptions {
    63→		if m.subscriptions[i].ID == id {
    64→			m.updated = sub
    65→			return nil
    66→		}
    67→	}
    68→	return fmt.Errorf("not found")
    69→}
    70→
    71→func (m *mockSubscriptionsStore) DeleteSubscription(_ context.Context, id string) error {
    72→	if m.deleteErr != nil {
    73→		return m.deleteErr
    74→	}
    75→	for _, s := range m.subscriptions {
    76→		if s.ID == id {
    77→			m.deleted = id
    78→			return nil
    79→		}
    80→	}
    81→	return fmt.Errorf("not found")
    82→}
    83→
    84→func setupSubscriptionsMux(store SubscriptionsStore) *http.ServeMux {
    85→	h := NewSubscriptionsHandler(store)
    86→	mux := http.NewServeMux()
    87→	mux.HandleFunc("GET /subscriptions", h.List)
    88→	mux.HandleFunc("POST /subscriptions", h.Create)
    89→	mux.HandleFunc("GET /subscriptions/{id}", h.Get)
    90→	mux.HandleFunc("PUT /subscriptions/{id}", h.Update)
    91→	mux.HandleFunc("DELETE /subscriptions/{id}", h.Delete)
    92→	return mux
    93→}
    94→
    95→func TestSubscriptionsHandlerList(t *testing.T) {
    96→	srcID := "s1"
    97→	projID := "p1"
    98→	store := &mockSubscriptionsStore{
    99→		subscriptions: []models.Subscription{
   100→			{ID: "sub-1", ChannelID: "ch1", Type: "source", SourceID: &srcID, CreatedAt: time.Now()},
   101→			{ID: "sub-2", ChannelID: "ch2", Type: "project", ProjectID: &projID, CreatedAt: time.Now()},
   102→		},
   103→	}
   104→	mux := setupSubscriptionsMux(store)
   105→
   106→	w := httptest.NewRecorder()
   107→	r := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, "/subscriptions", nil)
   108→	mux.ServeHTTP(w, r)
   109→
   110→	if w.Code != http.StatusOK {
   111→		t.Fatalf("expected status 200, got %d", w.Code)
   112→	}
   113→
   114→	var got struct {
   115→		Data []models.Subscription `json:"data"`
   116→		Meta struct {
   117→			Page    int `json:"page"`
   118→			PerPage int `json:"per_page"`
   119→			Total   int `json:"total"`
   120→		} `json:"meta"`
   121→	}
   122→	if err := json.NewDecoder(w.Body).Decode(&got); err != nil {
   123→		t.Fatalf("decode: %v", err)
   124→	}
   125→	if len(got.Data) != 2 {
   126→		t.Fatalf("expected 2 subscriptions, got %d", len(got.Data))
   127→	}
   128→	if got.Meta.Total != 2 {
   129→		t.Fatalf("expected total=2, got %d", got.Meta.Total)
   130→	}
   131→}

     1→package api
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"encoding/json"
     6→	"net/http"
     7→	"strconv"
     8→)
     9→
    10→type contextKey string
    11→
    12→const requestIDKey contextKey = "request_id"
    13→
    14→func withRequestID(ctx context.Context, id string) context.Context {
    15→	return context.WithValue(ctx, requestIDKey, id)
    16→}
    17→
    18→func getRequestID(ctx context.Context) string {
    19→	if id, ok := ctx.Value(requestIDKey).(string); ok {
    20→		return id
    21→	}
    22→	return ""
    23→}
    24→
    25→type meta struct {
    26→	RequestID string `json:"request_id"`
    27→}
    28→
    29→type listMeta struct {
    30→	RequestID string `json:"request_id"`
    31→	Page      int    `json:"page"`
    32→	PerPage   int    `json:"per_page"`
    33→	Total     int    `json:"total"`
    34→}
    35→
    36→type envelope struct {
    37→	Data any  `json:"data"`
    38→	Meta meta `json:"meta"`
    39→}
    40→
    41→type listEnvelope struct {
    42→	Data any      `json:"data"`
    43→	Meta listMeta `json:"meta"`
    44→}
    45→
    46→type apiError struct {
    47→	Code    string `json:"code"`
    48→	Message string `json:"message"`
    49→}
    50→
    51→type errorEnvelope struct {
    52→	Error apiError `json:"error"`
    53→	Meta  meta     `json:"meta"`
    54→}
    55→
    56→func RespondJSON(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, status int, data any) {
    57→	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
    58→	w.WriteHeader(status)
    59→	json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(envelope{
    60→		Data: data,
    61→		Meta: meta{RequestID: getRequestID(r.Context())},
    62→	})
    63→}
    64→
    65→func RespondList(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, status int, data any, page, perPage, total int) {
    66→	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
    67→	w.WriteHeader(status)
    68→	json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(listEnvelope{
    69→		Data: data,
    70→		Meta: listMeta{RequestID: getRequestID(r.Context()), Page: page, PerPage: perPage, Total: total},
    71→	})
    72→}
    73→
    74→func RespondError(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, status int, code, message string) {
    75→	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
    76→	w.WriteHeader(status)
    77→	json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(errorEnvelope{
    78→		Error: apiError{Code: code, Message: message},
    79→		Meta:  meta{RequestID: getRequestID(r.Context())},
    80→	})
    81→}
    82→
    83→func ParsePagination(r *http.Request) (page, perPage int) {
    84→	page = 1
    85→	perPage = 25
    86→	if v := r.URL.Query().Get("page"); v != "" {
    87→		if p, err := strconv.Atoi(v); err == nil && p > 0 {
    88→			page = p
    89→		}
    90→	}
    91→	if v := r.URL.Query().Get("per_page"); v != "" {
    92→		if pp, err := strconv.Atoi(v); err == nil && pp > 0 && pp <= 100 {
    93→			perPage = pp
    94→		}
    95→	}
    96→	return
    97→}
    98→
    99→func DecodeJSON(r *http.Request, dst any) error {
   100→	return json.NewDecoder(r.Body).Decode(dst)
   101→}
   102→

))}\n            </SelectContent>\n          </Select>\n        </div>\n      )}\n      {type === \"project\" && !isEditing && (\n        <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n          <div className=\"flex <SelectContent>\n                {projectsData?.data.map((p) => (\n                  <SelectItem key={p.id} value={p.id}>{p.name}</SelectItem>\n                ))}\n              </SelectContent>\n            </Select>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n            <Label>Source</Label>\n            <Select value={sourceId} onValueChange={setSourceId} required>\n setSourceProjectFilter(v); setSelectedSourceIds([]); }}>\n              <SelectTrigger><SelectValue placeholder=\"Select project\" /></SelectTrigger>\n              <SelectContent>\n      1→# API Reference — Changelogue
     2→
     3→RESTful HTTP API serving the Next.js dashboard, agent orchestration, and external integrations. Pure Go stdlib `net/http` with Go 1.22+ enhanced `ServeMux` — zero routing dependencies.
     4→
     5→**Key decisions:**
     6→- API key authentication (bearer token), `NO_AUTH=true` for development
     7→- SSE real-time events backed by PostgreSQL LISTEN/NOTIFY
     8→- Versioned prefix: `/api/v1`
     9→- All entity IDs are UUIDs (string type)
    10→- Sources and context sources are nested under projects
    11→- Two subscription types: `source` (per-source) and `project` (covers all sources)
    12→- Agent runs are triggered via API and processed asynchronously
    13→
    14→---
    15→
    16→## 1. Resource Endpoints
    17→
    18→### Projects (the central entity)
    19→
    20→A project represents a tracked piece of software. It owns sources, context sources, and semantic releases.
    21→
    22→| Method   | Path                      | Description                                    |
    23→|----------|---------------------------|------------------------------------------------|
    24→| `GET`    | `/api/v1/projects`        | List projects (paginated)                      |
    25→| `POST`   | `/api/v1/projects`        | Create a project                               |
    26→| `GET`    | `/api/v1/projects/{id}`   | Get project details                            |
    27→| `PUT`    | `/api/v1/projects/{id}`   | Update project metadata                        |
    28→| `DELETE` | `/api/v1/projects/{id}`   | Delete project (cascades to sources + subscriptions) |
    29→
    30→### Sources (nested under projects)

    85→| `GET`  | `/api/v1/projects/{projectId}/agent/runs`     | List agent runs for a project            |
    86→| `GET`  | `/api/v1/agent-runs/{id}`                     | Get single agent run details             |
    87→
    88→### Subscriptions (full CRUD)
    89→
    90→Subscriptions link a notification channel to either a specific source or an entire project. Two types:
    91→- `source` — triggers on releases from a specific source (requires `source_id`)
    92→- `project` — triggers on semantic releases for the project (requires `project_id`)
    93→
    94→| Method   | Path                          | Description              |
    95→|----------|-------------------------------|--------------------------|
    96→| `GET`    | `/api/v1/subscriptions`       | List all subscriptions   |
    97→| `POST`   | `/api/v1/subscriptions`       | Create a subscription    |
    98→| `GET`    | `/api/v1/subscriptions/{id}`  | Get single subscription  |
    99→| `PUT`    | `/api/v1/subscriptions/{id}`  | Update a subscription    |
   100→| `DELETE` | `/api/v1/subscriptions/{id}`  | Delete a subscription    |
   101→
   102→### Notification Channels
   103→
   104→| Method   | Path                            | Description                              |
   105→|----------|---------------------------------|------------------------------------------|
   106→| `GET`    | `/api/v1/channels`              | List registered notification channels    |
   107→| `POST`   | `/api/v1/channels`              | Register a new channel (Slack, Discord, webhook) |
   108→| `GET`    | `/api/v1/channels/{id}`         | Get channel details                      |
   109→| `PUT`    | `/api/v1/channels/{id}`         | Update channel config                    |
   110→| `DELETE` | `/api/v1/channels/{id}`         | Remove a channel                         |
   111→| `POST`   | `/api/v1/channels/{id}/test`    | Send a test notification to verify channel |
   112→
   113→### Providers (metadata)
   114→
   115→| Method | Path                 | Description                                      |
   116→|--------|----------------------|--------------------------------------------------|
   117→| `GET`  | `/api/v1/providers`  | List supported source types (`dockerhub`, `github`, etc.) |
   118→
   119→### System
   120→
   121→| Method | Path                    | Description                                              |
   122→|--------|-------------------------|----------------------------------------------------------|
   123→| `GET`  | `/api/v1/health`        | Health check (DB connectivity) — **public**              |
   124→| `GET`  | `/api/v1/stats`         | Dashboard stats (total releases, active sources, projects, pending agent runs) |
   125→| `GET`  | `/api/v1/stats/trend`   | Time-bucketed release counts (configurable granularity)  |

   289→If `trigger` is omitted, it defaults to `"manual"`.
   290→
   291→### Subscription (request body for POST/PUT)
   292→
   293→```json
   294→{
   295→  "channel_id": "aa0e8400-e29b-41d4-a716-446655440005",
   296→  "type": "source",
   297→  "source_id": "660e8400-e29b-41d4-a716-446655440001",
   298→  "version_filter": "^\\d+\\.\\d+\\.0$"
   299→}
   300→```
   301→
   302→Or for project-level subscriptions:
   303→
   304→```json
   305→{
   306→  "channel_id": "aa0e8400-e29b-41d4-a716-446655440005",
   307→  "type": "project",
   308→  "project_id": "880e8400-e29b-41d4-a716-446655440003",
   309→  "version_filter": ""
   310→}
   311→```
   312→
   313→The `type` must be either `"source"` or `"project"`. When `type` is `"source"`, `source_id` is required. When `type` is `"project"`, `project_id` is required. Response includes `id` (UUID), `created_at` fields.
   314→
   315→### Notification Channel (request body for POST/PUT)
   316→
   317→```json
   318→{
   319→  "type": "slack",
   320→  "name": "Engineering Releases",
   321→  "config": {
   322→    "webhook_url": "https://hooks.slack.com/services/...",
   323→    "channel": "#releases"
   324→  }
   325→}
   326→```
   327→
   328→Response includes `id` (UUID), `created_at`, `updated_at` fields. The `config` object is provider-specific:
   329→

   401→"use client";

   403→import { useState } from "react";

   413→import type { Subscription, SubscriptionInput, BatchSubscriptionInput } from "@/lib/api/types";
   414→
   415→interface SubscriptionFormProps {
   416→  initial?: Subscription;
   417→  onSubmit: (input: SubscriptionInput) => Promise<void>;
   418→  onBatchSubmit?: (input: BatchSubscriptionInput) => Promise<void>;
   419→  title: string;
   420→  onSuccess?: () => void;
   421→  onCancel?: () => void;

   423→
   424→export function SubscriptionForm({ initial, onSubmit, onBatchSubmit, title, onSuccess, onCancel }: SubscriptionFormProps) {
   425→  const router = useRouter();
   426→  const isEditing = !!initial;
   427→  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

   430→  const [channelId, setChannelId] = useState(initial?.channel_id ?? "");
   431→  // Single-select state (edit mode)

   433→  const [sourceId, setSourceId] = useState(initial?.source_id ?? "");
   434→  // Multi-select state (create mode)
   435→  const [selectedProjectIds, setSelectedProjectIds] = useState<string[]>([]);
   436→  const [selectedSourceIds, setSelectedSourceIds] = useState<string[]>([]);
   437→  // For source type: project filter
   438→  const [sourceProjectFilter, setSourceProjectFilter] = useState("");
   439→  const [versionFilter, setVersionFilter] = useState(initial?.version_filter ?? "");
   440→
   441→  const { data: projectsData } = useSWR("projects-for-sub", () => projectsApi.list(1, 100));
   442→  const { data: channelsData } = useSWR("channels-for-sub", () => channelsApi.list());
   443→  const { data: sourcesData } = useSWR(
   444→    type === "source" && (isEditing ? projectId : sourceProjectFilter)
   445→      ? `sources-for-sub-${isEditing ? projectId : sourceProjectFilter}`
   446→      : null,
   447→    () => sourcesApi.listByProject(isEditing ? projectId : sourceProjectFilter)
   448→  );
   449→
   450→  const toggleProjectId = (id: string) => {
   451→    setSelectedProjectIds((prev) =>
   452→      prev.includes(id) ? prev.filter((x) => x !== id) : [...prev, id]
   453→    );
   454→  };
   455→
   456→  const toggleSourceId = (id: string) => {
   457→    setSelectedSourceIds((prev) =>
   458→      prev.includes(id) ? prev.filter((x) => x !== id) : [...prev, id]
   459→    );
   460→  };
   461→
   462→  const allProjectIds = projectsData?.data.map((p) => p.id) ?? [];
   463→  const allSourceIds = sourcesData?.data.map((s) => s.id) ?? [];
   464→
   465→  const toggleAllProjects = () => {
   466→    if (selectedProjectIds.length === allProjectIds.length) {
   467→      setSelectedProjectIds([]);
   468→    } else {
   469→      setSelectedProjectIds([...allProjectIds]);
   470→    }
   471→  };
   472→
   473→  const toggleAllSources = () => {

   475→      setSelectedSourceIds([]);
   476→    } else {
   477→      setSelectedSourceIds([...allSourceIds]);
   478→    }
   479→  };
   480→
   481→  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
   482→    e.preventDefault();
   483→    setError("");
   484→    setSaving(true);
   485→    try {
   486→      if (isEditing) {
   487→        await onSubmit({
   488→          channel_id: channelId,
   489→          type,
   490→          source_id: type === "source" ? sourceId : undefined,
   491→          project_id: type === "project" ? projectId : undefined,
   492→          version_filter: versionFilter || undefined,
   493→        });
   494→      } else if (onBatchSubmit) {

   496→          channel_id: channelId,
   497→          type,
   498→          project_ids: type === "project" ? selectedProjectIds : undefined,
   499→          source_ids: type === "source" ? selectedSourceIds : undefined,
   500→          version_filter: versionFilter || undefined,
   501→        });
   502→      }
   503→      if (onSuccess) {
   504→        onSuccess();
   505→      } else {
   506→        router.push("/subscriptions");
   507→      }
   508→    } catch (err: unknown) {

   512→    }
   513→  };

   518→      <div className="space-y-2">

   521→          <SelectTrigger><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger>
   522→          <SelectContent>
   523→            <SelectItem value="project">Project</SelectItem>
   524→            <SelectItem value="source">Source</SelectItem>
   525→          </SelectContent>
   526→        </Select>
   527→      </div>
   528→
   529→      {/* PROJECT SELECTION */}
   530→      {type === "project" && isEditing && (

   532→          <Label>Project</Label>
   533→          <Select value={projectId} onValueChange={setProjectId} required>
   534→            <SelectTrigger><SelectValue placeholder="Select project" /></SelectTrigger>
   535→            <SelectContent>
   536→              {projectsData?.data.map((p) => (
   537→                <SelectItem key={p.id} value={p.id}>{p.name}</SelectItem>
   538→              ))}
   539→            </SelectContent>
   540→          </Select>
   541→        </div>
   542→      )}
   543→      {type === "project" && !isEditing && (
   544→        <div className="space-y-2">
   545→          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
   546→            <Label>Projects</Label>
   547→            <button
   548→              type="button"
   549→              onClick={toggleAllProjects}
   550→              className="text-xs text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800"
   551→            >
   552→              {selectedProjectIds.length === allProjectIds.length ? "Deselect all" : "Select all"}
   553→            </button>
   554→          </div>
   555→          <div className="max-h-48 overflow-y-auto rounded-md border border-input p-2 space-y-1">
   556→            {projectsData?.data.map((p) => (
   557→              <label key={p.id} className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded px-2 py-1.5 hover:bg-accent cursor-pointer">
   558→                <Checkbox
   559→                  checked={selectedProjectIds.includes(p.id)}
   560→                  onCheckedChange={() => toggleProjectId(p.id)}
   561→                />
   562→                <span className="text-sm">{p.name}</span>
   563→              </label>
   564→            ))}
   565→            {(!projectsData?.data || projectsData.data.length === 0) && (

   567→            )}
   568→          </div>
   569→          {selectedProjectIds.length > 0 && (
   570→            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{selectedProjectIds.length} selected</p>
   571→          )}
   572→        </div>
   573→      )}
   574→
   575→      {/* SOURCE SELECTION */}
   576→      {type === "source" && isEditing && (
   577→        <>
   578→          <div className="space-y-2">
   579→            <Label>Project (to list sources)</Label>
   580→            <Select value={projectId} onValueChange={(v) => { setProjectId(v); setSourceId(""); }}>
   581→              <SelectTrigger><SelectValue placeholder="Select project" /></SelectTrigger>
   582→              <SelectContent>
   583→                {projectsData?.data.map((p) => (
   584→                  <SelectItem key={p.id} value={p.id}>{p.name}</SelectItem>
   585→                ))}
   586→              </SelectContent>
   587→            </Select>
   588→          </div>
   589→          <div className="space-y-2">
   590→            <Label>Source</Label>
   591→            <Select value={sourceId} onValueChange={setSourceId} required>
   592→              <SelectTrigger><SelectValue placeholder="Select source" /></SelectTrigger>
   593→              <SelectContent>
   594→                {sourcesData?.data.map((s) => (
   595→                  <SelectItem key={s.id} value={s.id}>{s.provider}: {s.repository}</SelectItem>
   596→                ))}
   597→              </SelectContent>
   598→            </Select>
   599→          </div>
   600→        </>
   601→      )}
   602→      {type === "source" && !isEditing && (
   603→        <>
   604→          <div className="space-y-2">
   605→            <Label>Project (to list sources)</Label>
   606→            <Select value={sourceProjectFilter} onValueChange={(v) => { setSourceProjectFilter(v); setSelectedSourceIds([]); }}>
   607→              <SelectTrigger><SelectValue placeholder="Select project" /></SelectTrigger>
   608→              <SelectContent>
   609→                {projectsData?.data.map((p) => (
   610→                  <SelectItem key={p.id} value={p.id}>{p.name}</SelectItem>

   612→              </SelectContent>
   613→            </Select>

   618→                <Label>Sources</Label>
   619→                {allSourceIds.length > 0 && (

   621→                    type="button"

   623→                    className="text-xs text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800"
   624→                  >

   628→              </div>

   630→                {sourcesData?.data.map((s) => (

     1→# Multi-Select Subscription Creation — Implementation Plan
     2→
     3→> **For Claude:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task.
     4→
     5→**Goal:** Allow creating multiple subscriptions at once by selecting multiple projects/sources in the create form, backed by a batch API endpoint.
     6→
     7→**Architecture:** Add a `POST /api/v1/subscriptions/batch` endpoint that creates N subscriptions in a single transaction. The frontend subscription form switches to multi-select mode when creating (not editing). No DB schema changes.

   155→### Task 2: Implement the batch create handler and store interface
   156→

   162→**Step 1: Add to `internal/api/subscriptions.go`**

   164→Add `CreateSubscriptionBatch` to the `SubscriptionsStore` interface:
   165→
   166→```go
   167→type SubscriptionsStore interface {
   168→	ListSubscriptions(ctx context.Context, page, perPage int) ([]models.Subscription, int, error)
   169→	CreateSubscription(ctx context.Context, sub *models.Subscription) error
   170→	CreateSubscriptionBatch(ctx context.Context, subs []models.Subscription) ([]models.Subscription, error)
   171→	GetSubscription(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.Subscription, error)
   172→	UpdateSubscription(ctx context.Context, id string, sub *models.Subscription) error
   173→	DeleteSubscription(ctx context.Context, id string) error
   174→}
   175→```
   176→
   177→Add the input struct and handler after the `Delete` handler:
   178→
   179→```go
   180→// BatchSubscriptionInput is the request body for POST /subscriptions/batch.
   181→type BatchSubscriptionInput struct {
   182→	ChannelID     string   `json:"channel_id"`
   183→	Type          string   `json:"type"`
   184→	ProjectIDs    []string `json:"project_ids"`
   185→	SourceIDs     []string `json:"source_ids"`
   186→	VersionFilter string   `json:"version_filter,omitempty"`
   187→}
   188→
   189→// BatchCreate handles POST /subscriptions/batch — creates multiple subscriptions at once.
   190→func (h *SubscriptionsHandler) BatchCreate(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   191→	var input BatchSubscriptionInput
   192→	if err := DecodeJSON(r, &input); err != nil {
   193→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Invalid JSON body")
   194→		return
   195→	}
   196→	input.Type = strings.TrimSpace(input.Type)
   197→	input.ChannelID = strings.TrimSpace(input.ChannelID)
   198→	if input.Type != "source" && input.Type != "project" {
   199→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "validation_error", "type must be 'source' or 'project'")
   200→		return
   201→	}
   202→	if input.ChannelID == "" {
   203→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "validation_error", "channel_id is required")
   204→		return
   205→	}
   206→
   207→	var subs []models.Subscription
   208→	if input.Type == "project" {
   209→		if len(input.ProjectIDs) == 0 {
   210→			RespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "validation_error", "project_ids must not be empty when type is 'project'")
   211→			return
   212→		}
   213→		for _, pid := range input.ProjectIDs {
   214→			id := pid
   215→			subs = append(subs, models.Subscription{
   216→				ChannelID:     input.ChannelID,
   217→				Type:          input.Type,
   218→				ProjectID:     &id,
   219→				VersionFilter: input.VersionFilter,
   220→			})
   221→		}
   222→	} else {
   223→		if len(input.SourceIDs) == 0 {
   224→			RespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "validation_error", "source_ids must not be empty when type is 'source'")
   225→			return
   226→		}
   227→		for _, sid := range input.SourceIDs {
   228→			id := sid
   229→			subs = append(subs, models.Subscription{
   230→				ChannelID:     input.ChannelID,
   231→				Type:          input.Type,
   232→				SourceID:      &id,
   233→				VersionFilter: input.VersionFilter,
   234→			})
   235→		}
   236→	}
   237→
   238→	created, err := h.store.CreateSubscriptionBatch(r.Context(), subs)
   239→	if err != nil {
   240→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, "internal_error", "Failed to create subscriptions")
   241→		return
   242→	}
   243→	RespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusCreated, created)
   244→}
   245→```
   246→
   247→**Step 2: Add `CreateSubscriptionBatch` to `internal/api/pgstore.go`**
   248→
   249→Add after the existing `CreateSubscription` method (around line 323):
   250→
   251→```go
   252→func (s *PgStore) CreateSubscriptionBatch(ctx context.Context, subs []models.Subscription) ([]models.Subscription, error) {
   253→	tx, err := s.pool.Begin(ctx)
   254→	if err != nil {
   255→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("begin tx: %w", err)
   256→	}
   257→	defer tx.Rollback(ctx)
   258→
   259→	var result []models.Subscription
   260→	for i := range subs {
   261→		sub := subs[i]
   262→		err := tx.QueryRow(ctx,
   263→			`INSERT INTO subscriptions (channel_id, type, source_id, project_id, version_filter)
   264→			 VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5)
   265→			 RETURNING id, created_at`,
   266→			sub.ChannelID, sub.Type, sub.SourceID, sub.ProjectID, sub.VersionFilter,
   267→		).Scan(&sub.ID, &sub.CreatedAt)
   268→		if err != nil {
   269→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("insert subscription %d: %w", i, err)
   270→		}
   271→		result = append(result, sub)
   272→	}
   273→
   274→	if err := tx.Commit(ctx); err != nil {
   275→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("commit tx: %w", err)
   276→	}
   277→	return result, nil
   278→}
   279→```
