   399→
   400→```tsx
   401→"use client";
   402→
   403→import { useState } from "react";
   404→import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
   405→import useSWR from "swr";
   406→import { projects as projectsApi, channels as channelsApi, sources as sourcesApi } from "@/lib/api/client";
   407→import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
   408→import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
   409→import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
   410→import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
   411→import { Checkbox } from "@/components/ui/checkbox";
   412→import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "@/components/ui/select";
   413→import type { Subscription, SubscriptionInput, BatchSubscriptionInput } from "@/lib/api/types";
   414→
   415→interface SubscriptionFormProps {
   416→  initial?: Subscription;
   417→  onSubmit: (input: SubscriptionInput) => Promise<void>;
   418→  onBatchSubmit?: (input: BatchSubscriptionInput) => Promise<void>;
   419→  title: string;
   420→  onSuccess?: () => void;
   421→  onCancel?: () => void;
   422→}
   423→
   424→export function SubscriptionForm({ initial, onSubmit, onBatchSubmit, title, onSuccess, onCancel }: SubscriptionFormProps) {
   425→  const router = useRouter();
   426→  const isEditing = !!initial;
   427→  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
   428→  const [error, setError] = useState("");
   429→  const [type, setType] = useState<"source" | "project">(initial?.type ?? "project");
   430→  const [channelId, setChannelId] = useState(initial?.channel_id ?? "");
   431→  // Single-select state (edit mode)
   432→  const [projectId, setProjectId] = useState(initial?.project_id ?? "");
   433→  const [sourceId, setSourceId] = useState(initial?.source_id ?? "");
   434→  // Multi-select state (create mode)
   435→  const [selectedProjectIds, setSelectedProjectIds] = useState<string[]>([]);
   436→  const [selectedSourceIds, setSelectedSourceIds] = useState<string[]>([]);
   437→  // For source type: project filter
   438→  const [sourceProjectFilter, setSourceProjectFilter] = useState("");
   439→  const [versionFilter, setVersionFilter] = useState(initial?.version_filter ?? "");
   440→
   441→  const { data: projectsData } = useSWR("projects-for-sub", () => projectsApi.list(1, 100));
   442→  const { data: channelsData } = useSWR("channels-for-sub", () => channelsApi.list());
   443→  const { data: sourcesData } = useSWR(
   444→    type === "source" && (isEditing ? projectId : sourceProjectFilter)
   445→      ? `sources-for-sub-${isEditing ? projectId : sourceProjectFilter}`
   446→      : null,
   447→    () => sourcesApi.listByProject(isEditing ? projectId : sourceProjectFilter)
   448→  );
   449→
   450→  const toggleProjectId = (id: string) => {
   451→    setSelectedProjectIds((prev) =>
   452→      prev.includes(id) ? prev.filter((x) => x !== id) : [...prev, id]
   453→    );
   454→  };
   455→
   456→  const toggleSourceId = (id: string) => {
   457→    setSelectedSourceIds((prev) =>
   458→      prev.includes(id) ? prev.filter((x) => x !== id) : [...prev, id]
   459→    );
   460→  };
   461→
   462→  const allProjectIds = projectsData?.data.map((p) => p.id) ?? [];
   463→  const allSourceIds = sourcesData?.data.map((s) => s.id) ?? [];
   464→
   465→  const toggleAllProjects = () => {
   466→    if (selectedProjectIds.length === allProjectIds.length) {
   467→      setSelectedProjectIds([]);
   468→    } else {
   469→      setSelectedProjectIds([...allProjectIds]);
   470→    }
   471→  };
   472→
   473→  const toggleAllSources = () => {
   474→    if (selectedSourceIds.length === allSourceIds.length) {
   475→      setSelectedSourceIds([]);
   476→    } else {
   477→      setSelectedSourceIds([...allSourceIds]);
   478→    }
   479→  };
   480→
   481→  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
   482→    e.preventDefault();
   483→    setError("");
   484→    setSaving(true);
   485→    try {
   486→      if (isEditing) {
   487→        await onSubmit({
   488→          channel_id: channelId,
   489→          type,
   490→          source_id: type === "source" ? sourceId : undefined,
   491→          project_id: type === "project" ? projectId : undefined,
   492→          version_filter: versionFilter || undefined,
   493→        });
   494→      } else if (onBatchSubmit) {
   495→        await onBatchSubmit({
   496→          channel_id: channelId,
   497→          type,
   498→          project_ids: type === "project" ? selectedProjectIds : undefined,
   499→          source_ids: type === "source" ? selectedSourceIds : undefined,
   500→          version_filter: versionFilter || undefined,
   501→        });
   502→      }
   503→      if (onSuccess) {
   504→        onSuccess();
   505→      } else {
   506→        router.push("/subscriptions");
   507→      }
   508→    } catch (err: unknown) {
   509→      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to save");
   510→    } finally {
   511→      setSaving(false);
   512→    }
   513→  };
   514→
   515→  const formContent = (
   516→    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
   517→      {error && <div className="rounded-md bg-red-50 p-3 text-sm text-red-700">{error}</div>}
   518→      <div className="space-y-2">
   519→        <Label>Subscription Type</Label>
   520→        <Select value={type} onValueChange={(v) => { setType(v as "source" | "project"); setSelectedProjectIds([]); setSelectedSourceIds([]); }}>
   521→          <SelectTrigger><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger>
   522→          <SelectContent>
   523→            <SelectItem value="project">Project</SelectItem>
   524→            <SelectItem value="source">Source</SelectItem>
   525→          </SelectContent>
   526→        </Select>
   527→      </div>
   528→
   529→      {/* PROJECT SELECTION */}
   530→      {type === "project" && isEditing && (
   531→        <div className="space-y-2">
   532→          <Label>Project</Label>
   533→          <Select value={projectId} onValueChange={setProjectId} required>
   534→            <SelectTrigger><SelectValue placeholder="Select project" /></SelectTrigger>
   535→            <SelectContent>
   536→              {projectsData?.data.map((p) => (
   537→                <SelectItem key={p.id} value={p.id}>{p.name}</SelectItem>
   538→              ))}
   539→            </SelectContent>
   540→          </Select>
   541→        </div>
   542→      )}
   543→      {type === "project" && !isEditing && (
   544→        <div className="space-y-2">
   545→          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
   546→            <Label>Projects</Label>
   547→            <button
   548→              type="button"
   549→              onClick={toggleAllProjects}
   550→              className="text-xs text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800"
   551→            >
   552→              {selectedProjectIds.length === allProjectIds.length ? "Deselect all" : "Select all"}
   553→            </button>
   554→          </div>
   555→          <div className="max-h-48 overflow-y-auto rounded-md border border-input p-2 space-y-1">
   556→            {projectsData?.data.map((p) => (
   557→              <label key={p.id} className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded px-2 py-1.5 hover:bg-accent cursor-pointer">
   558→                <Checkbox
   559→                  checked={selectedProjectIds.includes(p.id)}
   560→                  onCheckedChange={() => toggleProjectId(p.id)}
   561→                />
   562→                <span className="text-sm">{p.name}</span>
   563→              </label>
   564→            ))}
   565→            {(!projectsData?.data || projectsData.data.length === 0) && (
   566→              <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground px-2 py-1">No projects found</p>
   567→            )}
   568→          </div>
   569→          {selectedProjectIds.length > 0 && (
   570→            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{selectedProjectIds.length} selected</p>
   571→          )}
   572→        </div>
   573→      )}
   574→
   575→      {/* SOURCE SELECTION */}
   576→      {type === "source" && isEditing && (
   577→        <>
   578→          <div className="space-y-2">
   579→            <Label>Project (to list sources)</Label>
   580→            <Select value={projectId} onValueChange={(v) => { setProjectId(v); setSourceId(""); }}>
   581→              <SelectTrigger><SelectValue placeholder="Select project" /></SelectTrigger>
   582→              <SelectContent>
   583→                {projectsData?.data.map((p) => (
   584→                  <SelectItem key={p.id} value={p.id}>{p.name}</SelectItem>
   585→                ))}
   586→              </SelectContent>
   587→            </Select>
   588→          </div>
   589→          <div className="space-y-2">
   590→            <Label>Source</Label>
   591→            <Select value={sourceId} onValueChange={setSourceId} required>
   592→              <SelectTrigger><SelectValue placeholder="Select source" /></SelectTrigger>
   593→              <SelectContent>
   594→                {sourcesData?.data.map((s) => (
   595→                  <SelectItem key={s.id} value={s.id}>{s.provider}: {s.repository}</SelectItem>
   596→                ))}
   597→              </SelectContent>
   598→            </Select>
   599→          </div>
   600→        </>
   601→      )}
   602→      {type === "source" && !isEditing && (
   603→        <>
   604→          <div className="space-y-2">
   605→            <Label>Project (to list sources)</Label>
   606→            <Select value={sourceProjectFilter} onValueChange={(v) => { setSourceProjectFilter(v); setSelectedSourceIds([]); }}>
   607→              <SelectTrigger><SelectValue placeholder="Select project" /></SelectTrigger>
   608→              <SelectContent>
   609→                {projectsData?.data.map((p) => (
   610→                  <SelectItem key={p.id} value={p.id}>{p.name}</SelectItem>
   611→                ))}
   612→              </SelectContent>
   613→            </Select>
   614→          </div>
   615→          {sourceProjectFilter && (
   616→            <div className="space-y-2">
   617→              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
   618→                <Label>Sources</Label>
   619→                {allSourceIds.length > 0 && (
   620→                  <button
   621→                    type="button"
   622→                    onClick={toggleAllSources}
   623→                    className="text-xs text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800"
   624→                  >
   625→                    {selectedSourceIds.length === allSourceIds.length ? "Deselect all" : "Select all"}
   626→                  </button>
   627→                )}
   628→              </div>
   629→              <div className="max-h-48 overflow-y-auto rounded-md border border-input p-2 space-y-1">
   630→                {sourcesData?.data.map((s) => (
   631→                  <label key={s.id} className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded px-2 py-1.5 hover:bg-accent cursor-pointer">
   632→                    <Checkbox
   633→                      checked={selectedSourceIds.includes(s.id)}
   634→                      onCheckedChange={() => toggleSourceId(s.id)}
   635→                    />
   636→                    <span className="text-sm">{s.provider}: {s.repository}</span>
   637→                  </label>
   638→                ))}
   639→                {(!sourcesData?.data || sourcesData.data.length === 0) && (
   640→                  <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground px-2 py-1">No sources found</p>
   641→                )}
   642→              </div>
   643→              {selectedSourceIds.length > 0 && (
   644→                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{selectedSourceIds.length} selected</p>
   645→              )}
   646→            </div>
   647→          )}
   648→        </>
   649→      )}
   650→
   651→      <div className="space-y-2">
   652→        <Label>Notification Channel</Label>
   653→        <Select value={channelId} onValueChange={setChannelId} required>
   654→          <SelectTrigger><SelectValue placeholder="Select channel" /></SelectTrigger>
   655→          <SelectContent>
   656→            {channelsData?.data.map((ch) => (
   657→              <SelectItem key={ch.id} value={ch.id}>{ch.name} ({ch.type})</SelectItem>
   658→            ))}
   659→          </SelectContent>
   660→        </Select>
   661→      </div>
   662→      <div className="space-y-2">
   663→        <Label htmlFor="version_filter">Version Filter (regex, optional)</Label>
   664→        <Input id="version_filter" value={versionFilter} onChange={(e) => setVersionFilter(e.target.value)} placeholder='e.g. ^v\d+\.\d+\.\d+$' />
   665→      </div>
   666→      <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
   667→        <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={() => onCancel ? onCancel() : router.back()}>Cancel</Button>
   668→        <Button type="submit" disabled={saving}>{saving ? "Saving..." : "Save"}</Button>
   669→      </div>
   670→    </form>
   671→  );
   672→
   673→  if (onSuccess) {
   674→    return formContent;
   675→  }
   676→
   677→  return (
   678→    <Card className="mx-auto max-w-2xl">
   679→      <CardHeader><CardTitle>{title}</CardTitle></CardHeader>
   680→      <CardContent>
   681→        {formContent}
   682→      </CardContent>
   683→    </Card>
   684→  );
   685→}
   686→```
   687→
   688→**Step 2: Verify TypeScript compiles**
   689→
   690→Run: `cd web && npx tsc --noEmit`
   691→Expected: No errors.
   692→
   693→**Step 3: Commit**
   694→

   696→git add web/components/subscriptions/subscription-form.tsx
   697→git commit -m "feat(frontend): multi-select projects/sources in subscription create form"

```json
{
  "description": "Implement batch subscription backend",
  "subagent_type": "general-purpose",
  "prompt": "You are implementing batch subscription creation for a Go backend. Here is the full task spec:\n\n## Context\nThis is a Go project (module: github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue). The subscription system currently creates one subscription at a time. We need to add a batch creation endpoint.\n\n## What to do\n\n### 1. Modify `internal/api/subscriptions.go`\n\n**Update the SubscriptionsStore interface** (currently at lines 12-18) to add CreateSubscriptionBatch:\n\n```go\ntype SubscriptionsStore interface {\n\tListSubscriptions(ctx context.Context, page, perPage int) ([]models.Subscription, int, error)\n\tCreateSubscription(ctx context.Context, sub *models.Subscription) error\n\tCreateSubscriptionBatch(ctx context.Context, subs []models.Subscription) ([]models.Subscription, error)\n\tGetSubscription(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.Subscription, error)\n\tUpdateSubscription(ctx context.Context, id string, sub *models.Subscription) error\n\tDeleteSubscription(ctx context.Context, id string) error\n}\n```\n\n**Add BatchSubscriptionInput struct and BatchCreate handler** after the Delete handler (after line 143):\n\n```go\n// BatchSubscriptionInput is the request body for POST /subscriptions/batch.\ntype BatchSubscriptionInput struct {\n\tChannelID     string   `json:\"channel_id\"`\n\tType          string   `json:\"type\"`\n\tProjectIDs    []string `json:\"project_ids\"`\n\tSourceIDs     []string `json:\"source_ids\"`\n\tVersionFilter string   `json:\"version_filter,omitempty\"`\n}\n\n// BatchCreate handles POST /subscriptions/batch — creates multiple subscriptions at once.\nfunc (h *SubscriptionsHandler) BatchCreate(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\tvar input BatchSubscriptionInput\n\tif err := DecodeJSON(r, &input); err != nil {\n\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, \"bad_request\", \"Invalid JSON body\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tinput.Type = strings.TrimSpace(input.Type)\n\tinput.ChannelID = strings.TrimSpace(input.ChannelID)\n\tif input.Type != \"source\" && input.Type != \"project\" {\n\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, \"validation_error\", \"type must be 'source' or 'project'\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tif input.ChannelID == \"\" {\n\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, \"validation_error\", \"channel_id is required\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tvar subs []models.Subscription\n\tif input.Type == \"project\" {\n\t\tif len(input.ProjectIDs) == 0 {\n\t\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, \"validation_error\", \"project_ids must not be empty when type is 'project'\")\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor _, pid := range input.ProjectIDs {\n\t\t\tid := pid\n\t\t\tsubs = append(subs, models.Subscription{\n\t\t\t\tChannelID:     input.ChannelID,\n\t\t\t\tType:          input.Type,\n\t\t\t\tProjectID:     &id,\n\t\t\t\tVersionFilter: input.VersionFilter,\n\t\t\t})\n\t\t}\n\t} else {\n\t\tif len(input.SourceIDs) == 0 {\n\t\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, \"validation_error\", \"source_ids must not be empty when type is 'source'\")\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor _, sid := range input.SourceIDs {\n\t\t\tid := sid\n\t\t\tsubs = append(subs, models.Subscription{\n\t\t\t\tChannelID:     input.ChannelID,\n\t\t\t\tType:          input.Type,\n\t\t\t\tSourceID:      &id,\n\t\t\t\tVersionFilter: input.VersionFilter,\n\t\t\t})\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tcreated, err := h.store.CreateSubscriptionBatch(r.Context(), subs)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, \"internal_error\", \"Failed to create subscriptions\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tRespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusCreated, created)\n}\n```\n\n### 2. Modify `internal/api/pgstore.go`\n\nAdd `CreateSubscriptionBatch` method after the existing `CreateSubscription` method (after line 323):\n\n```go\nfunc (s *PgStore) CreateSubscriptionBatch(ctx context.Context, subs []models.Subscription) ([]models.Subscription, error) {\n\ttx, err := s.pool.Begin(ctx)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"begin tx: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer tx.Rollback(ctx)\n\n\tvar result []models.Subscription\n\tfor i := range subs {\n\t\tsub := subs[i]\n\t\terr := tx.QueryRow(ctx,\n\t\t\t`INSERT INTO subscriptions (channel_id, type, source_id, project_id, version_filter)\n\t\t\t VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5)\n\t\t\t RETURNING id, created_at`,\n\t\t\tsub.ChannelID, sub.Type, sub.SourceID, sub.ProjectID, sub.VersionFilter,\n\t\t).Scan(&sub.ID, &sub.CreatedAt)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"insert subscription %d: %w\", i, err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tresult = append(result, sub)\n\t}\n\n\tif err := tx.Commit(ctx); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"commit tx: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn result, nil\n}\n```\n\n### 3. Modify `internal/api/server.go`\n\nAdd this line after line 84 (after `POST /api/v1/subscriptions`):\n\n```go\n\tmux.Handle(\"POST /api/v1/subscriptions/batch\", chain(http.HandlerFunc(subscriptions.BatchCreate)))\n```\n\n### 4. Modify `internal/api/subscriptions_test.go`\n\n**Add to mockSubscriptionsStore struct** (after existing fields, around line 23):\n```go\n\tbatchCreated []models.Subscription\n\tbatchErr     error\n```\n\n**Add mock method:**\n```go\nfunc (m *mockSubscriptionsStore) CreateSubscriptionBatch(_ context.Context, subs []models.Subscription) ([]models.Subscription, error) {\n\tif m.batchErr != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, m.batchErr\n\t}\n\tvar result []models.Subscription\n\tfor i, sub := range subs {\n\t\tsub.ID = fmt.Sprintf(\"sub-batch-%d\", i)\n\t\tsub.CreatedAt = time.Date(2026, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)\n\t\tresult = append(result, sub)\n\t}\n\tm.batchCreated = result\n\treturn result, nil\n}\n```\n\n**Update setupSubscriptionsMux** to add the batch route:\n```go\nfunc setupSubscriptionsMux(store SubscriptionsStore) *http.ServeMux {\n\th := NewSubscriptionsHandler(store)\n\tmux := http.NewServeMux()\n\tmux.HandleFunc(\"GET /subscriptions\", h.List)\n\tmux.HandleFunc(\"POST /subscriptions\", h.Create)\n\tmux.HandleFunc(\"POST /subscriptions/batch\", h.BatchCreate)\n\tmux.HandleFunc(\"GET /subscriptions/{id}\", h.Get)\n\tmux.HandleFunc(\"PUT /subscriptions/{id}\", h.Update)\n\tmux.HandleFunc(\"DELETE /subscriptions/{id}\", h.Delete)\n\treturn mux\n}\n```\n\n**Add 4 test functions at the end of the file:**\n\n```go\nfunc TestSubscriptionsHandlerBatchCreateProjects(t *testing.T) {\n\tstore := &mockSubscriptionsStore{}\n\tmux := setupSubscriptionsMux(store)\n\n\tbody := `{\"channel_id\":\"ch1\",\"type\":\"project\",\"project_ids\":[\"p1\",\"p2\",\"p3\"]}`\n\tw := httptest.NewRecorder()\n\tr := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodPost, \"/subscriptions/batch\", strings.NewReader(body))\n\tr.Header.Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n\tmux.ServeHTTP(w, r)\n\n\tif w.Code != http.StatusCreated {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"expected status 201, got %d; body: %s\", w.Code, w.Body.String())\n\t}\n\tvar got struct {\n\t\tData []models.Subscription `json:\"data\"`\n\t}\n\tif err := json.NewDecoder(w.Body).Decode(&got); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"decode: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif len(got.Data) != 3 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"expected 3 subscriptions, got %d\", len(got.Data))\n\t}\n\tif len(store.batchCreated) != 3 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"expected 3 in store, got %d\", len(store.batchCreated))\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestSubscriptionsHandlerBatchCreateSources(t *testing.T) {\n\tstore := &mockSubscriptionsStore{}\n\tmux := setupSubscriptionsMux(store)\n\n\tbody := `{\"channel_id\":\"ch1\",\"type\":\"source\",\"source_ids\":[\"s1\",\"s2\"]}`\n\tw := httptest.NewRecorder()\n\tr := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodPost, \"/subscriptions/batch\", strings.NewReader(body))\n\tr.Header.Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n\tmux.ServeHTTP(w, r)\n\n\tif w.Code != http.StatusCreated {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"expected status 201, got %d; body: %s\", w.Code, w.Body.String())\n\t}\n\tvar got struct {\n\t\tData []models.Subscription `json:\"data\"`\n\t}\n\tif err := json.NewDecoder(w.Body).Decode(&got); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"decode: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif len(got.Data) != 2 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"expected 2 subscriptions, got %d\", len(got.Data))\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestSubscriptionsHandlerBatchCreateEmptyIDs(t *testing.T) {\n\tstore := &mockSubscriptionsStore{}\n\tmux := setupSubscriptionsMux(store)\n\n\tbody := `{\"channel_id\":\"ch1\",\"type\":\"project\",\"project_ids\":[]}`\n\tw := httptest.NewRecorder()\n\tr := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodPost, \"/subscriptions/batch\", strings.NewReader(body))\n\tr.Header.Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n\tmux.ServeHTTP(w, r)\n\n\tif w.Code != http.StatusUnprocessableEntity {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"expected status 422, got %d\", w.Code)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestSubscriptionsHandlerBatchCreateMissingChannelID(t *testing.T) {\n\tstore := &mockSubscriptionsStore{}\n\tmux := setupSubscriptionsMux(store)\n\n\tbody := `{\"type\":\"project\",\"project_ids\":[\"p1\"]}`\n\tw := httptest.NewRecorder()\n\tr := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodPost, \"/subscriptions/batch\", strings.NewReader(body))\n\tr.Header.Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n\tmux.ServeHTTP(w, r)\n\n\tif w.Code != http.StatusUnprocessableEntity {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"expected status 422, got %d\", w.Code)\n\t}\n}\n```\n\n### 5. Run tests\n\nAfter all changes, run: `go test -v -run TestSubscriptionsHandler ./internal/api/...`\n\nAll tests (existing + new batch tests) must pass.\n\n### 6. Commit\n\n```bash\ngit add internal/api/subscriptions.go internal/api/pgstore.go internal/api/server.go internal/api/subscriptions_test.go\ngit commit -m \"feat(api): add batch subscription creation endpoint\n\nAdd POST /api/v1/subscriptions/batch to create multiple subscriptions\nin a single transaction. Accepts channel_id, type, and an array of\nproject_ids or source_ids.\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.6 <noreply@anthropic.com>\"\n```\n\nIMPORTANT: Read each file BEFORE editing. All changes are committed. Here is a summary of what was done across the four files:
```

```
### Files Modified

**`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/subscriptions.go`**
- Added `CreateSubscriptionBatch` to the `SubscriptionsStore` interface.
- Added the `BatchSubscriptionInput` struct for the request body.
- Added the `BatchCreate` handler method that validates input (type must be `source` or `project`, `channel_id` required, appropriate IDs array must be non-empty) and delegates to the store.

**`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/pgstore.go`**
- Added `CreateSubscriptionBatch` method on `PgStore` that wraps all inserts in a single database transaction, rolling back on any failure.

**`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/server.go`**
- Registered the route `POST /api/v1/subscriptions/batch` pointing to `subscriptions.BatchCreate`.

**`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/subscriptions_test.go`**
- Added `batchCreated` and `batchErr` fields to the mock store.
- Added `CreateSubscriptionBatch` mock method.
- Added the batch route to `setupSubscriptionsMux`.
- Added four test functions: `TestSubscriptionsHandlerBatchCreateProjects`, `TestSubscriptionsHandlerBatchCreateSources`, `TestSubscriptionsHandlerBatchCreateEmptyIDs`, and `TestSubscriptionsHandlerBatchCreateMissingChannelID`.

All 16 tests (12 existing + 4 new) pass. Commit `a4913ec` created on the `main` branch.
```

```
Both changes are complete and committed. Here is the summary:

## Changes Made

### Task 1: Rewrote `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/subscriptions/subscription-form.tsx`

The subscription form now supports two modes:

- **Edit mode** (`isEditing = true`): Single-select dropdowns for project or source, same as before.
- **Create mode** (`isEditing = false`): Multi-select checkboxes with "Select all / Deselect all" toggle, selection count display, and scrollable list containers. Uses the `onBatchSubmit` callback with `BatchSubscriptionInput`.

Key additions:
- Imported `Checkbox` component and `BatchSubscriptionInput` type
- Added `onBatchSubmit` prop to the form interface
- Added multi-select state (`selectedProjectIds`, `selectedSourceIds`) and toggle functions
- For source type in create mode, added a separate `sourceProjectFilter` state to pick a project before selecting sources
- The `handleSubmit` function branches: edit mode calls `onSubmit`, create mode calls `onBatchSubmit`

### Task 2: Updated `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/app/subscriptions/page.tsx`

- Added `BatchSubscriptionInput` to the type import (line 11)
- Added `onBatchSubmit` prop to the `SubscriptionForm` inside the create dialog, wiring it to `subsApi.batchCreate(input)`

### Verification

`npx tsc --noEmit` passed with zero errors.

### Commit
```

> AGENT

All tasks complete. Now to address your question:

**"What if the subscription is source level instead of project level?"**

Both types are fully supported! The multi-select form handles source-level subscriptions too:

1. **In the create form**: when you select type "Source", you first pick a project (to filter sources), then a scrollable checkbox list of that project's sources appears. You can select multiple sources and click "Select all". All selected sources get batch-created as individual subscriptions to the chosen channel.

2. **In the batch API**: send `type: "source"` with `source_ids` array:
   ```json
   {
     "channel_id": "...",
     "type": "source",
     "source_ids": ["s1", "s2", "s3"]
   }
   ```

3. **Edit mode**: still single-select for both types (you're editing one existing subscription).

Here's a summary of all commits made:

```
a4913ec feat(api): add batch subscription creation endpoint
a329e51 feat(ui): add shadcn checkbox component
c5418e9 feat(frontend): add batch subscription API types and client method
6fb2787 feat(frontend): multi-select subscription creation form
6fa75e8 docs: add batch subscription endpoint to API.md
```

```
     3→import { useState } from "react";

     9→import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
    10→import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
    11→import { Checkbox } from "@/components/ui/checkbox";

    14→
    15→interface SubscriptionFormProps {
    16→  initial?: Subscription;
    17→  onSubmit: (input: SubscriptionInput) => Promise<void>;
    18→  onBatchSubmit?: (input: BatchSubscriptionInput) => Promise<void>;
    19→  title: string;

    21→  onCancel?: () => void;
    22→}
    23→
    24→export function SubscriptionForm({ initial, onSubmit, onBatchSubmit, title, onSuccess, onCancel }: SubscriptionFormProps) {
    25→  const router = useRouter();
    26→  const isEditing = !!initial;
    27→  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
    28→  const [error, setError] = useState("");
    29→  const [type, setType] = useState<"source" | "project">(initial?.type ?? "project");
    30→  const [channelId, setChannelId] = useState(initial?.channel_id ?? "");
    31→  // Single-select state (edit mode)
    32→  const [projectId, setProjectId] = useState(initial?.project_id ?? "");
    33→  const [sourceId, setSourceId] = useState(initial?.source_id ?? "");
    34→  // Multi-select state (create mode)
    35→  const [selectedProjectIds, setSelectedProjectIds] = useState<string[]>([]);
    36→  const [selectedSourceIds, setSelectedSourceIds] = useState<string[]>([]);
    37→  // For source type: project filter
    38→  const [sourceProjectFilter, setSourceProjectFilter] = useState("");
    39→  const [versionFilter, setVersionFilter] = useState(initial?.version_filter ?? "");
    40→
    41→  const { data: projectsData } = useSWR("projects-for-sub", () => projectsApi.list(1, 100));
    42→  const { data: channelsData } = useSWR("channels-for-sub", () => channelsApi.list());
    43→  const { data: sourcesData } = useSWR(
    44→    type === "source" && (isEditing ? projectId : sourceProjectFilter)
    45→      ? `sources-for-sub-${isEditing ? projectId : sourceProjectFilter}`
    46→      : null,
    47→    () => sourcesApi.listByProject(isEditing ? projectId : sourceProjectFilter)
    48→  );
    49→
    50→  const toggleProjectId = (id: string) => {
    51→    setSelectedProjectIds((prev) =>
    52→      prev.includes(id) ? prev.filter((x) => x !== id) : [...prev, id]
    53→    );
    54→  };
    55→
    56→  const toggleSourceId = (id: string) => {
    57→    setSelectedSourceIds((prev) =>
    58→      prev.includes(id) ? prev.filter((x) => x !== id) : [...prev, id]
    59→    );
    60→  };
    61→
    62→  const allProjectIds = projectsData?.data.map((p) => p.id) ?? [];
    63→  const allSourceIds = sourcesData?.data.map((s) => s.id) ?? [];
    64→
    65→  const toggleAllProjects = () => {
    66→    if (selectedProjectIds.length === allProjectIds.length) {
    67→      setSelectedProjectIds([]);
    68→    } else {
    69→      setSelectedProjectIds([...allProjectIds]);
    70→    }
    71→  };
    72→
    73→  const toggleAllSources = () => {
    74→    if (selectedSourceIds.length === allSourceIds.length) {
    75→      setSelectedSourceIds([]);
    76→    } else {
    77→      setSelectedSourceIds([...allSourceIds]);
    78→    }
    79→  };
    80→
    81→  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    82→    e.preventDefault();
    83→    setError("");
    84→    setSaving(true);
    85→    try {
    86→      if (isEditing) {
    87→        await onSubmit({
    88→          channel_id: channelId,
    89→          type,
    90→          source_id: type === "source" ? sourceId : undefined,
    91→          project_id: type === "project" ? projectId : undefined,
    92→          version_filter: versionFilter || undefined,
    93→        });
    94→      } else if (onBatchSubmit) {
    95→        await onBatchSubmit({
    96→          channel_id: channelId,
    97→          type,
    98→          project_ids: type === "project" ? selectedProjectIds : undefined,
    99→          source_ids: type === "source" ? selectedSourceIds : undefined,
   100→          version_filter: versionFilter || undefined,
   101→        });
   102→      }
   103→      if (onSuccess) {
   104→        onSuccess();
   105→      } else {
   106→        router.push("/subscriptions");
   107→      }
   108→    } catch (err: unknown) {
   109→      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to save");
   110→    } finally {
   111→      setSaving(false);
   112→    }
   113→  };
   114→
   115→  const formContent = (
   116→    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
   117→      {error && <div className="rounded-md bg-red-50 p-3 text-sm text-red-700">{error}</div>}
   118→      <div className="space-y-2">
   119→        <Label>Subscription Type</Label>
   120→        <Select value={type} onValueChange={(v) => { setType(v as "source" | "project"); setSelectedProjectIds([]); setSelectedSourceIds([]); }}>
   121→          <SelectTrigger><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger>
   122→          <SelectContent>
   123→            <SelectItem value="project">Project</SelectItem>
   124→            <SelectItem value="source">Source</SelectItem>
   125→          </SelectContent>
   126→        </Select>
   127→      </div>
   128→
   129→      {/* PROJECT SELECTION */}
   130→      {type === "project" && isEditing && (
   131→        <div className="space-y-2">
   132→          <Label>Project</Label>
   133→          <Select value={projectId} onValueChange={setProjectId} required>
   134→            <SelectTrigger><SelectValue placeholder="Select project" /></SelectTrigger>
   135→            <SelectContent>
   136→              {projectsData?.data.map((p) => (
   137→                <SelectItem key={p.id} value={p.id}>{p.name}</SelectItem>
   138→              ))}
   139→            </SelectContent>
   140→          </Select>
   141→        </div>
   142→      )}
   143→      {type === "project" && !isEditing && (
   144→        <div className="space-y-2">
   145→          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
   146→            <Label>Projects</Label>
   147→            <button
   148→              type="button"
   149→              onClick={toggleAllProjects}
   150→              className="text-xs text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800"
   151→            >
   152→              {selectedProjectIds.length === allProjectIds.length ? "Deselect all" : "Select all"}
   153→            </button>
   154→          </div>
   155→          <div className="max-h-48 overflow-y-auto rounded-md border border-input p-2 space-y-1">
   156→            {projectsData?.data.map((p) => (
   157→              <label key={p.id} className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded px-2 py-1.5 hover:bg-accent cursor-pointer">
   158→                <Checkbox
   159→                  checked={selectedProjectIds.includes(p.id)}
   160→                  onCheckedChange={() => toggleProjectId(p.id)}
   161→                />
   162→                <span className="text-sm">{p.name}</span>
   163→              </label>
   164→            ))}
   165→            {(!projectsData?.data || projectsData.data.length === 0) && (
   166→              <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground px-2 py-1">No projects found</p>
   167→            )}
   168→          </div>
   169→          {selectedProjectIds.length > 0 && (
   170→            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{selectedProjectIds.length} selected</p>
   171→          )}
   172→        </div>
   173→      )}
   174→
   175→      {/* SOURCE SELECTION */}
   176→      {type === "source" && isEditing && (
   177→        <>
   178→          <div className="space-y-2">
   179→            <Label>Project (to list sources)</Label>
   180→            <Select value={projectId} onValueChange={(v) => { setProjectId(v); setSourceId(""); }}>
   181→              <SelectTrigger><SelectValue placeholder="Select project" /></SelectTrigger>
   182→              <SelectContent>
   183→                {projectsData?.data.map((p) => (
   184→                  <SelectItem key={p.id} value={p.id}>{p.name}</SelectItem>
   185→                ))}
   186→              </SelectContent>
   187→            </Select>
   188→          </div>
   189→          <div className="space-y-2">
   190→            <Label>Source</Label>
   191→            <Select value={sourceId} onValueChange={setSourceId} required>
   192→              <SelectTrigger><SelectValue placeholder="Select source" /></SelectTrigger>
   193→              <SelectContent>
   194→                {sourcesData?.data.map((s) => (
   195→                  <SelectItem key={s.id} value={s.id}>{s.provider}: {s.repository}</SelectItem>
   196→                ))}
   197→              </SelectContent>
   198→            </Select>
   199→          </div>
   200→        </>
   201→      )}
   202→      {type === "source" && !isEditing && (
   203→        <>
   204→          <div className="space-y-2">
   205→            <Label>Project (to list sources)</Label>
   206→            <Select value={sourceProjectFilter} onValueChange={(v) => { setSourceProjectFilter(v); setSelectedSourceIds([]); }}>
   207→              <SelectTrigger><SelectValue placeholder="Select project" /></SelectTrigger>
   208→              <SelectContent>
   209→                {projectsData?.data.map((p) => (
   210→                  <SelectItem key={p.id} value={p.id}>{p.name}</SelectItem>
   211→                ))}
   212→              </SelectContent>
   213→            </Select>
   214→          </div>
   215→          {sourceProjectFilter && (
   216→            <div className="space-y-2">
   217→              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
   218→                <Label>Sources</Label>
   219→                {allSourceIds.length > 0 && (

   221→                    type="button"
   222→                    onClick={toggleAllSources}
   223→                    className="text-xs text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800"
   224→                  >
   225→                    {selectedSourceIds.length === allSourceIds.length ? "Deselect all" : "Select all"}
```

```json
  "new_string": "  const [selectedSourceIds, setSelectedSourceIds] = useState<string[]>([]);\n  const [versionFilter, setVersionFilter] = useState(initial?.version_filter ?? \"\");\n\n  const { data: projectsData } = useSWR(\"projects-for-sub\", () => projectsApi.list(1, 100));\n  const { data: channelsData } = useSWR(\"channels-for-sub\", () => channelsApi.list());\n\n  // Edit mode: fetch sources for the selected project\n  const { data: sourcesData } = useSWR(\n    type === \"source\" && isEditing && projectId\n      ? `sources-for-sub-${projectId}`\n      : null,\n    () => sourcesApi.listByProject(projectId)\n  );\n\n  // Create mode (source type): fetch sources for ALL projects\n  const projectIds = projectsData?.data.map((p) => p.id) ?? [];\n  const { data: allSourcesData } = useSWR(\n    type === \"source\" && !isEditing && projectIds.length > 0\n      ? `all-sources-for-sub-${projectIds.join(\",\")}`\n      : null,\n    async () => {\n      const results = await Promise.all(\n        projectIds.map((pid) => sourcesApi.listByProject(pid).catch(() => ({ data: [] as Source[] })))\n      );\n      return results.flatMap((r) => r.data);\n    }\n  );\n\n  // Group all sources by project for display\n  const sourcesByProject = useMemo(() => {\n    if (!allSourcesData || !projectsData?.data) return [];\n    const projectMap = new Map(projectsData.data.map((p) => [p.id, p.name]));\n    const groups = new Map<string, { projectName: string; sources: Source[] }>();\n    for (const s of allSourcesData) {\n      const name = projectMap.get(s.project_id) ?? s.project_id;\n      if (!groups.has(s.project_id)) {\n        groups.set(s.project_id, { projectName: name, sources: [] });\n      }\n      groups.get(s.project_id)!.sources.push(s);\n    }\n    return Array.from(groups.values());\n  }, [allSourcesData, projectsData]);"
```

```
   162→// RunMigrations applies River's schema and the application schema. Idempotent — safe to call on every startup.
   163→func RunMigrations(ctx context.Context, pool *pgxpool.Pool) error {
   164→	migrator, err := rivermigrate.New(riverpgxv5.New(pool), nil)
   165→	if err != nil {
   166→		return fmt.Errorf("create river migrator: %w", err)
   167→	}

   169→		return fmt.Errorf("river migrations: %w", err)
   170→	}
   171→
   172→	if _, err := pool.Exec(ctx, schema); err != nil {
   173→		return fmt.Errorf("app migrations: %w", err)
   174→	}
   175→	return nil
   176→}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/pc/.claude/plans/streamed-puzzling-micali.md",
  "content": "# Rename Subscription Types: source→source_release, project→semantic_release\n\n## Context\n\nThe current subscription type values `\"source\"` and `\"project\"` are confusing — they describe what entity you're subscribing to, not what you receive. Users expect to choose between \"Source Releases\" (raw release notifications) and \"Semantic Releases\" (AI-analyzed reports). Renaming to `source_release` / `semantic_release` makes the intent clear.\n\n## Scope\n\nRename the subscription `type` column values across the full stack: DB schema + migration, Go handlers, Go store, Go tests, notification routing, agent orchestrator, frontend types/form/page, API docs.\n\n**Old → New:**\n- `\"source\"` → `\"source_release\"`\n- `\"project\"` → `\"semantic_release\"`\n\n## Files to Modify\n\n### Backend (Go)\n1. `internal/db/migrations.go` — Schema CHECK constraints + data migration UPDATE\n2. `internal/api/subscriptions.go` — Validation checks, error messages, batch logic\n3. `internal/api/pgstore.go` — SQL WHERE clauses in `ListSourceSubscriptions` and `ListProjectSubscriptions`\n4. `internal/api/subscriptions_test.go` — All test data and assertions\n5. `internal/api/server.go` — No changes needed (routes don't reference type values)\n6. `internal/routing/worker.go` — No changes needed (calls store methods, doesn't check type values directly)\n7. `internal/routing/worker_test.go` — Test data Type fields\n8. `internal/agent/orchestrator.go` — No changes needed (calls store methods)\n9. `internal/agent/orchestrator_test.go` — Mock test data Type fields\n\n### Frontend\n10. `web/lib/api/types.ts` — TypeScript union types\n11. `web/lib/api/client.ts` — No changes needed (doesn't reference type values)\n12. `web/components/subscriptions/subscription-form.tsx` — Form state, conditionals, select items, labels\n13. `web/app/subscriptions/page.tsx` — Badge color map keys, conditional display\n\n### Docs\n14. `API.md` — Type descriptions and example request bodies\n\n## Implementation Steps\n\n### Step 1: Update DB schema (`internal/db/migrations.go`)\n\nChange the schema string:\n- Line 92 comment: `source-level and project-level` → `source releases and semantic releases`\n- Line 96: `CHECK (type IN ('source', 'project'))` → `CHECK (type IN ('source_release', 'semantic_release'))`\n- Lines 102-103: `type = 'source'` → `type = 'source_release'`, `type = 'project'` → `type = 'semantic_release'`\n\nAdd a post-schema migration block in `RunMigrations` (after schema exec) to migrate existing data:\n```sql\nUPDATE subscriptions SET type = 'source_release' WHERE type = 'source';\nUPDATE subscriptions SET type = 'semantic_release' WHERE type = 'project';\nALTER TABLE subscriptions DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS subscriptions_type_check;\nALTER TABLE subscriptions ADD CONSTRAINT subscriptions_type_check CHECK (type IN ('source_release', 'semantic_release'));\n```\n\nNote: Since schema uses `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS`, existing tables won't have their CHECK updated by the schema. The ALTER statements handle this. The UPDATE + ALTER approach is safe because:\n- Fresh DBs get the new CHECK from CREATE TABLE\n- Existing DBs get data migrated + constraint swapped by ALTER\n\n### Step 2: Update Go handler (`internal/api/subscriptions.go`)\n\nReplace all `\"source\"` → `\"source_release\"` and `\"project\"` → `\"semantic_release\"` in:\n- Validation checks (4 occurrences in Create, 4 in Update, 3 in BatchCreate)\n- Error messages mentioning the type values\n- Conditional logic (`input.Type == \"project\"` → `input.Type == \"semantic_release\"`)\n\n### Step 3: Update Go store queries (`internal/api/pgstore.go`)\n\n- Line ~700: `WHERE type = 'source'` → `WHERE type = 'source_release'`\n- Line ~848: `WHERE type = 'project'` → `WHERE type = 'semantic_release'`\n\n### Step 4: Update Go tests (`internal/api/subscriptions_test.go`)\n\nAll test structs and JSON bodies: replace `Type: \"source\"` → `Type: \"source_release\"`, `\"type\":\"source\"` → `\"type\":\"source_release\"`, etc.\n\n### Step 5: Update routing and agent test data\n\n- `internal/routing/worker_test.go`: All `Type: \"source\"` → `Type: \"source_release\"`\n- `internal/agent/orchestrator_test.go`: No type string literals to change (uses store method)\n\n### Step 6: Update frontend types (`web/lib/api/types.ts`)\n\nChange all three union types: `\"source\" | \"project\"` → `\"source_release\" | \"semantic_release\"`\n\n### Step 7: Update subscription form (`web/components/subscriptions/subscription-form.tsx`)\n\n- State default: `\"project\"` → `\"semantic_release\"`\n- All conditionals: `type === \"source\"` → `type === \"source_release\"`, `type === \"project\"` → `type === \"semantic_release\"`\n- Select items: `value=\"project\"` → `value=\"semantic_release\"`, label \"Project\" → \"Semantic Release\"\n- Select items: `value=\"source\"` → `value=\"source_release\"`, label \"Source\" → \"Source Release\"\n\n### Step 8: Update subscriptions page (`web/app/subscriptions/page.tsx`)\n\n- Badge color map: `source:` → `source_release:`, `project:` → `semantic_release:`\n- Conditional: `s.type === \"source\"` → `s.type === \"source_release\"`, `sub.type === \"source\"` → `sub.type === \"source_release\"`\n\n### Step 9: Update API.md\n\n- Description of two types\n- Example request bodies\n\n### Step 10: Verify\n\n- `go test ./internal/api/...` — all tests pass\n- `go test ./internal/routing/...` — routing tests pass\n- `go build -o changelogue ./cmd/server` — compiles\n- `cd web && npx tsc --noEmit` — no TS errors\n- `cd web && npm run build` — frontend builds\n\n## Commit\n\nSingle commit: `refactor: rename subscription types source→source_release, project→semantic_release`\n"
}
```
